# Modlight web panel: the contract between the SPA and the Rust server

The web panel is a client-side app (`server/web`, Vite + React 19) that keeps
every upstream React component, and gets from the Rust server what upstream's
Next.js server layer used to produce: the HTML document, the data of each page
(translations included, as upstream sent them), and the results of the server
actions. This document is the
work order for that server side. It complements `PORT.md` (sections 7, 8, 11)
and, where the two differ in detail, this document is the more precise one.

Upstream (`upstream/Modlight`) stays the specification of behaviour. Every
statement below names the upstream code it comes from.

## 1. The SPA

### 1.1 Layout of `server/web`

| Path | What it is |
|---|---|
| `components/`, `hooks/`, `types/`, `lib/` (part), `app/globals.css`, `app/error.tsx`, `app/(app)/dashboard/error.tsx`, `app/s/[code]/unlock/unlock-form.tsx` | Byte-for-byte copies of upstream files, written by `scripts/sync-upstream.mjs`. `upstream.lock.json` holds the sha256 of each; `npm run sync:check` proves they are still verbatim. `@/x` resolves here, exactly as upstream's `@/*` path alias does. |
| `src/overrides/` | Browser versions of upstream modules that cannot run in a browser, at the same `@/` path (they win over the copies): client ports of the upstream Server Components under `components/` (13 files), the four auth forms with the optional Cloudflare Turnstile check added (`components/auth/{login,register,forgot,invite}-form.tsx`, not upstream: section 11), type mirrors of server-only `lib/` modules (generated by `scripts/gen-type-mirrors.mjs`), and `lib/config.ts` (the public part of upstream `config`). |
| `src/rpc/actions/<module>.ts` | One generated RPC stub per upstream `app/actions/<module>.ts` (`scripts/gen-actions.mjs`): same exports, same parameter and return types, exported types copied. `@/app/actions/*` resolves here. |
| `src/shims/` | `next/link`, `next/navigation`, `next/dynamic`, `next-intl` (on `use-intl` 4.13.0, the version next-intl 4.13.0 uses) and `client-only`. Any other `next/*`, `next-intl/*` or `server-only` import fails the build. |
| `src/pages/` | Ports of upstream `app/**/page.tsx` and `layout.tsx` (Server Components) as client components, mirroring the `app/` tree. Each exports a `...Props` type: the page data the server sends (section 6). |
| `src/routes/` | The react-router data router: route tree, the loader, error/loading boundaries, the 501 box, code splitting. |
| `src/runtime/` | Tagged JSON, the `/_page` and `/_rpc` clients, the translations the root layout delivers, navigation, Next-compatible redirect/not-found errors. |
| `src/contract/` | TypeScript types of the layout data, the route table (page ids as in `server/src/pages/table.rs`, plus the route handlers and files the server answers itself), and the drizzle row shapes that cross the wire. |
| `src/turnstile/` | Not upstream: the optional Cloudflare Turnstile widget (`widget.tsx`: script loader, `useCaptcha()`), the admin card (`TurnstileSettings.tsx`) and the stubs of the server's `turnstile` actions (`actions.ts`). Section 11. |
| `src/encryption/` | Not upstream: per-account file encryption (the Settings card, the unlock page, the library banner, the admin note, the stubs of the server's `encryption` actions). Section 11 and `docs/ENCRYPTION.md`. |
| `fixtures/` | Example payloads for every page (`fixtures/pages/*.json`), shared layout data (`fixtures/layouts.json`) and canned action answers (`fixtures/actions.json`). They double as worked examples of section 6. The root layout's `messages` are filled in from `server/messages/` (the server's own copy of upstream `messages/`). |
| `tests/` | vitest + jsdom: `smoke.test.tsx` mounts every fixture page; `runtime.test.tsx` pins the wire formats; `route-table.test.ts` checks the route table against `server/src/pages/table.rs` and the server's matching tests; `turnstile.test.tsx` the optional Turnstile widget and its header (section 11). |
| `scripts/` | `sync-upstream.mjs`, `gen-actions.mjs`, `gen-type-mirrors.mjs`, `audit-bundle.mjs`, `size-report.mjs`. |

Every file in `src/` that ports or mirrors upstream code names it with an
`@upstream <path>` tag; `upstream.lock.json` records the upstream hash each port
was written against, so `npm run sync:check` fails when upstream moves under a
port.

### 1.2 Build and run

Node is a build-time tool only. Use pnpm 10.33.0 (as upstream):

```sh
cd server/web
npx -y pnpm@10.33.0 install          # versions pinned by pnpm-lock.yaml (seeded from upstream's)
npm run build                        # tsc --noEmit, then vite build -> dist/
npm test                             # vitest: 42 smoke + 16 runtime + 3 route-table tests
npm run audit:bundle                 # after a build: no server code in dist/
npm run sync:check                   # copies verbatim, ports current
npm run verify                       # all of the above in one go (about 10 s)
npm run size                         # first-load sizes against the reference on :8420
npm run dev                          # vite on 127.0.0.1:5173, proxying to the Rust dev server :8421
npm run dev:fixtures                 # vite on 127.0.0.1:5173, no backend: fixtures/pages/*.json
```

After upstream changes: `npm run sync:upstream` (copies, rewrites the
manifest, warns about ports whose upstream moved), `npm run gen:actions` (RPC
stubs and `src/rpc/actions.manifest.json`), `npm run gen:mirrors` (type
mirrors), then update the ports `sync:check` names and accept them with
`node scripts/sync-upstream.mjs --accept`.

`dist/` is what the Rust server serves: `dist/index.html` (the document
template, section 2), `dist/assets/*` (hashed JS, CSS, fonts; immutable) and
`dist/.vite/manifest.json` (entry and chunk names, if the server wants to emit
preload hints).

Dev server: a document request (`Accept: text/html`) for a page, or for a URL
no route matches, gets vite's bare `index.html`, decided by the SPA's own route
table (`src/contract/routes.ts`); the SPA then fetches `/_page` for the first
page too. Every other request (`/_rpc`, `/_page`, the route handlers and files
of `SERVER_PATHS`: `/api/*`, `/{tag}/{code}/raw`, `/s/{code}`, `/avatar/*`,
`/bg/*`, `/embed/*`, `/oembed`, `/download/app`, `/icon.svg`..., and POST
`/upload`) is proxied to `MODLIGHT_RUST_URL` (default `http://127.0.0.1:8421`).
Opening `http://127.0.0.1:8421` directly serves `dist/` through the Rust shell
instead (rebuild with `npm run build`; the server rereads `index.html` when it
changes).

Fixture mode: `npm run dev:fixtures`, then open any page URL. The fixture is
chosen by the route the URL matches, or explicitly with
`?__fixture=<file name>` (e.g. `/x?__fixture=not-found`,
`/alicja/a.png?__fixture=media-gate`). Server actions answer from
`fixtures/actions.json` (`"<module>.<export>": <envelope>`) or with an error
envelope.

### 1.3 Size

`npm run size` (gzip -9, every script and stylesheet of a first visit with an
empty cache; upstream measured on the reference build at `127.0.0.1:8420`,
signed in for the dashboard rows):

| Page | SPA raw / gzip | Upstream raw / gzip | Upstream inline RSC |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/login` | 767 KB / 204 KB | 1077 KB / 289 KB | 142 KB |
| `/welcome` | 782 / 205 | 1072 / 287 | 181 |
| `/download` | 760 / 200 | 1066 / 285 | 136 |
| 404 / media page | 892 / 247 | 1048 / 278 | 133 |
| `/dashboard?all=1` | 1107 / 319 | 1440 / 400 | 164 |
| `/dashboard/profile` | 1068 / 306 | 1407 / 390 | 173 |

So the SPA's first load is 11 to 30% smaller (gzip). Whole build: 168 JS
chunks + 2 CSS files, about 2025 KB raw / 587 KB gzip for every route.
Translations are not code on either side: upstream puts the dictionary into
the RSC payload of each first page view (part of the "inline RSC" column), the
Rust server into the inlined page payload (`layouts.root.messages`, about
125 KB raw), and a client navigation repeats it on neither (section 4.1).

---

## 2. Serving the SPA: the HTML document

### 2.1 Which requests get the document

Every `GET`/`HEAD` for a **page URL** (section 6; the route table is
`src/contract/routes.ts`) answers with `dist/index.html`, filled in as below,
after the same request processing upstream applies to a page:

1. `proxy.ts`:
   - On the shortener host (`SHORTLINK_URL`'s hostname), a path other than `/`
     that is not in `PASSTHROUGH` is rewritten to `/s<path>` before routing.
     The SPA's own paths are in that passthrough list too (`/_page`, `/_rpc`,
     `/assets/`, as upstream has `_next`; done in the skeleton, SKELETON.md 9.4).
     A rewritten page URL is resolved as the rewritten path, but the document is
     served at the URL the browser asked for (the SPA renders whatever route
     `/_page` reports).
   - `/dashboard*` without a Better Auth session cookie: `307` to
     `/login?next=<pathname>` (pathname only, no query).
2. The page's own logic (section 6): `307` redirects (`Location` header, no
   body needed), `404` (not-found document), or the page.

Unknown paths that are neither a page nor a route handler get the not-found
document with status `404` (PORT.md section 6). A path ending in `/` (other
than `/` itself) is first answered with `308` to the same path without the
slash, query kept (Next's default `trailingSlash: false`; seen on the
reference: `/login/?x=1` -> `308 /login?x=1`). On `/_page` that is
`{"redirect": "/login?x=1", "status": 308}`.

Status codes, as the reference build answers them:

- `200` for a page;
- `307` with `Location` for a redirect from the proxy gate, from a layout, or
  from a page outside `/dashboard` (Next's `redirect()`; `308` would be
  `permanentRedirect()`, which upstream never calls);
- `404` for `notFound()` outside `/dashboard` and for unknown paths;
- **under `/dashboard`, a page's own `redirect()` or `notFound()` answers `200`**:
  `app/(app)/dashboard/loading.tsx` makes Next flush the shell before the page
  runs, so the status is already sent. For a redirect the reference sends the
  document with `<meta id="__next-page-redirect" http-equiv="refresh"
  content="1;url=<target>"/>` and the client replaces the URL (seen: a non-admin
  on `/dashboard/admin` gets `200` and lands on `/dashboard`); for `notFound` the
  head keeps the page's own title and adds `<meta name="robots"
  content="noindex"/>` (seen: `/dashboard/admin/users/<unknown>/files`). To
  match, answer `200` with the inlined payload being the redirect (or not-found)
  payload of section 4.2, plus that meta tag for a redirect: the SPA replaces the
  URL and loads the target, as upstream's client does. The skeleton answers a
  plain `307` / `404` there, which works with the SPA as well; it only differs in
  the status line (open question 12.4);
- `500` when computing the page failed (the error document, section 4.4);
- `501` while a page or layout has no loader in the Rust server yet (skeleton
  only; section 4.2).

Headers on documents: the `next.config.ts` rules for the path (security
headers, `appCsp`; `/authorize` gets `authorizeCsp` instead), plus
`Cache-Control: private, no-cache, no-store, max-age=0, must-revalidate` and
`Content-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8`, as the reference sends. No
`X-Powered-By`. `dist/assets/*` are content-hashed: serve them with
`Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`.

The production CSP works unchanged for the SPA: its scripts and styles are
same-origin files, the only inline script is the theme script below
(`'unsafe-inline'` is already in `script-src`), the page payload is
`type="application/json"` (never executed), and the bundle uses no `eval`
(checked by `scripts/audit-bundle.mjs`).

### 2.2 The document (`server/src/pages/shell.rs`)

The Rust shell takes `dist/index.html` and, for every page URL:

- sets `lang`, `data-mode` and `data-theme` on `<html>` (the template carries
  only `class="geist-sans geist-mono h-full antialiased"`: the stable names of
  the two `next/font` variable classes, `src/fonts/geist.css`, and upstream's
  `h-full antialiased`);
- drops the template's `<title>Modlight</title>` and inserts the rendered head
  (title, description, application-name, robots, Open Graph, Twitter,
  alternates, icons) before `</head>`;
- inserts `<script id="__MODLIGHT_PAGE__" type="application/json">` with the
  page payload (section 4, exactly what `/_page` would answer, escaped so it
  cannot end the script) before `</body>`.

What the template brings itself:

- next-themes' blocking script, the exact function `next-themes@0.4.6` emits for
  the root layout's `<ThemeProvider attribute="data-mode" defaultTheme={mode}
  enableSystem disableTransitionOnChange>`, with `defaultTheme` read from the
  `data-mode` the server put on `<html>` (= `resolveMode(ml_mode)`, upstream's
  argument). So nothing has to be injected into `<body>` besides the payload.
- no icon links: the server's head has them.

The SPA reads the payload once, loads the page's code and renders without
asking `/_page` again. A document without the payload or without the `<html>`
attributes still works: the SPA fetches `/_page` itself and fills `<html>` in
(that is the vite dev server).

**Two things the document must carry that the skeleton's shell does not yet:**

1. **The page's own skin and wallpaper** (open question 12.2). Upstream paints
   `data-theme` from the root layout and then, on pages that render
   `<ThemeApplier>`, a blocking inline script switches the skin (and the
   wallpaper) before the first paint. The SPA has no server-rendered body, so the
   document must carry the **final** values (otherwise the visitor's own skin
   shows until the SPA has loaded):
   - pages under `(app)`: `resolveTheme(layouts["(app)"].theme)` and
     `layouts["(app)"].background`;
   - `/{tag}/{code}` and `/{code}` (all three views): `resolveTheme(props.ownerTheme)`
     and `props.ownerBackground`;
   - `/a/{tag}/{slug}`, `/u/{tag}`: `resolveTheme(props.owner.theme)` and `props.background`;
   - `/s/{code}/unlock`, `/s/{tag}/{name}/unlock`: `resolveTheme(props.ownerTheme)`
     and `props.background`;
   - every other page and the not-found / error documents: `layouts.root.theme`,
     no wallpaper.

   A wallpaper is `style="--ml-bg-image:url(&quot;{url}&quot;);--ml-bg-dim:{dim}"`
   plus `data-bg="on"` on `<html>` (`dim` written as JavaScript's
   `String(number)`, e.g. `0.7`), what `ThemeApplier`'s script sets.
2. **`layouts.root.config`** (section 5.7; the skeleton does not send it yet; open
   question 12.1).

The production CSP needs nothing new: scripts and styles are same-origin files,
the two inline scripts are the theme script (`'unsafe-inline'` is already in
`script-src`) and the JSON payload (never executed), and the bundle uses no
`eval` (`scripts/audit-bundle.mjs` checks).

Optional, for speed only: `Link` preload headers for the two fonts and the
entry CSS (the reference sends them for its own files) and
`<link rel="modulepreload">` for the page's chunk, read from
`dist/.vite/manifest.json` (key `src/pages/<app path>/page.tsx`).

---

## 3. Tagged JSON

Every JSON body in both directions (page payloads, RPC arguments, RPC results)
is plain JSON plus four tags for values JSON cannot carry (PORT.md section 7):

| Value | Wire form |
|---|---|
| `Date` | `{"$date": "2026-10-18T12:00:00.000Z"}` (ISO 8601 with milliseconds, `Z`) |
| `undefined` | `{"$undefined": 1}` |
| `bigint` | `{"$bigint": "123"}` |
| `NaN`, `Infinity`, `-Infinity` | `{"$num": "NaN"}`, `{"$num": "Infinity"}`, `{"$num": "-Infinity"}` |

An object with exactly one key that is one of these tags is decoded as the
value; anything else is plain JSON. `undefined` survives inside objects and
arrays in both directions: the browser sends `{"locale": {"$undefined": 1}}`
for `updatePreferences({ locale: undefined })`, because upstream code tests
`"locale" in input` and React's server-action encoding keeps the key. In
results, a field that is absent and a field that is `undefined` are the same to
the SPA, so the server may simply omit `undefined` fields.

Where upstream passes a `Date` across (a Server Component prop of type `Date`,
or an action that returns drizzle rows), send `{"$date": ...}`; where upstream
converts to a string first (`createdAt.toISOString()`), send the string. The
types in section 6 and in `src/rpc/actions/*.ts` say which.

Implementation: `src/runtime/tagged-json.ts`; tests: `tests/runtime.test.tsx`.

---

## 4. Page data: `GET /_page`

Implemented by `server/src/pages/mod.rs`; this section is what the SPA relies on.

### 4.1 Request

```
GET /_page?path=<encodeURIComponent(location.pathname)>&<the page's own query string>
Accept: application/json
X-Modlight-Messages: <locale>:<messagesHash>      (once the tab holds a dictionary)
Cookie: ...
```

The **first** `path` parameter is the page's pathname, passed through
`encodeURIComponent` (so the server decodes it once and gets the browser's
percent-encoded pathname back); the page's own query string follows unchanged,
a second `path` included. `/dashboard?folder=a&page=2` is asked as
`/_page?path=%2Fdashboard&folder=a&page=2`; `/a%20b/c.png?w=64` as
`/_page?path=%2Fa%2520b%2Fc.png&w=64` (`src/runtime/payload.ts` `pageDataUrl`).

The server evaluates it as it would a document request for that URL (section
2.1: trailing slash, shortener rewrite, the `/dashboard` cookie gate), with the
request's own cookies and headers. Session refresh policy: none on `/_page`
(a client navigation is an `RSC: 1` request upstream, SKELETON.md 8).

`X-Modlight-Messages`: the SPA sends the locale and `messagesHash` of the
dictionary it last received; when they still match the request's locale and the
server's dictionary, the root layout answers `"messages": null` instead of the
~125 KB of translations (section 5.7).

### 4.2 Responses

Tagged JSON, `Content-Type: application/json`,
`Cache-Control: private, no-cache, no-store, max-age=0, must-revalidate`:

| Case | Status | Body | The SPA |
|---|---|---|---|
| a page | 200 | `{"route", "params", "layouts", "props", "meta"}` | renders it |
| `redirect()` (page, layout, proxy gate, trailing slash) | 200 | `{"redirect": "<url>", "status": 307 \| 308}` | client navigation to an SPA page, a document load otherwise |
| `notFound()`, or no page at this path | 200 | `{"notFound": true, "layouts": {"root": ...}, "meta": {...}}` | renders `app/not-found.tsx` inside the root layout |
| a route handler or a static file (`/s/{code}`, `/{tag}/{code}/raw`, `/download/app`, `/oembed`, ...) | 200 | `{"reload": true}` | loads the URL as a document |
| no loader yet for the page or one of its layouts (skeleton) | 501 | `{"notImplemented": true, "route", "file", "layouts"}` | a box naming `file` where the missing part renders, in every build (the Rust server serves the production bundle; a 501 only exists while the port is incomplete) |
| a loader failed | 500 | `{"error": {"message": "..."}, "layouts": {...those that loaded}}` | the nearest `error.tsx` (4.4) |
| anything else: no connection, not JSON, JSON without one of the keys above (the skeleton's `400 {"message": "Bad Request"}` for a malformed path) | any | | loads the URL as a document |

The last row and the unmatched case follow Next 16's client, which falls back
to a browser navigation when the RSC request fails, is not RSC, or is not a 200
(`client/components/router-reducer/fetch-server-response.js`). The reference
answers the RSC request of a URL no route matches with a 404 (verified
`/x/y/z/w`), and of a page that calls `notFound()` with a 200 (verified
`/u/nonexistent`). So the SPA loads URLs that match no route (or a route handler)
as documents without asking, and renders the not-found page in place only for a
page's own `notFound()`; the server's `notFound` answer for an unmatched URL
matters only for the vite dev server's first load. A 500 page payload is not a
fallback case: upstream sends a page's error inside a 200 RSC answer, rendered
by the error boundary. The SPA never reloads the document that is still loading
(the dev server's `index.html` would come straight back); that shows the error
page instead.

- `route`: the page's Next app path, route groups stripped, `[params]` kept, as
  in `server/src/pages/table.rs` and `src/contract/routes.ts`:
  `"/dashboard/folders"`, `"/login"`, `"/[userCode]/[mediaCode]"`,
  `"/s/[code]/unlock"`. When the server resolves a URL to another page than its
  shape suggests (the shortener rewrite), the SPA renders the page `route` names.
- `params`: the route's dynamic segments, decoded.
- `layouts`: the data of every layout on the route's path, outside in: always
  `root`, plus `"(app)"` or `"(auth)"` (sections 5.7 to 5.9).
- `props`: the page's data (section 6).
- `meta`: the resolved head, `{"title", "description", "applicationName",
  "robots"}` (`server/src/pages/meta.rs` `ResolvedHead`). The SPA sets
  `document.title` and the three named `<meta>` tags on every navigation, and
  drops the first document's Open Graph / Twitter / alternate tags once another
  page shows (they are for link-preview bots, which only read the first document).

### 4.3 When the SPA asks

- First page of a visit: never (the payload is inlined, section 2.2), except
  under the vite dev server, whose `index.html` has no payload.
- Every client navigation (links, `router.push/replace`, back/forward): one
  request for the new URL. Unlike Next, the layouts are part of every answer;
  the SPA always uses the newest (so the sidebar counts are fresh after a
  navigation, where Next would keep the old layout until a refresh).
- `router.refresh()`, and after every action whose envelope says `revalidate`:
  one request for the current URL.
- Not for route handlers, files and URLs no route matches: those are document
  loads (4.2).
- Once more, without `X-Modlight-Messages`, if an answer says `"messages": null`
  for a dictionary the tab does not hold (it cannot happen while the SPA only
  sends what it received; the retry keeps it safe), and if that answer has no
  dictionary either, a document load.

### 4.4 Errors

A failed loader answers `500` with `{"error": {"message": "..."}, "layouts":
{...}}`. In production the message is React's masked text (development: the
real message, as `next dev` shows it):

```
An error occurred in the Server Components render. The specific message is omitted in production builds to avoid leaking sensitive details. A digest property is included on this error instance which may provide additional details about the nature of the error.
```

An optional `"digest"` next to it is shown by the error pages as
"Reference: <digest>" (upstream's are numeric hashes; the skeleton sends none
for pages yet, and one for actions).

Which error page shows follows Next's `error.tsx` boundaries, decided by the
layouts that came back: a page under `/dashboard` whose `layouts["(app)"]`
loaded gets `app/(app)/dashboard/error.tsx` inside the panel shell; anything
else (another page, or a layout that failed) gets `app/error.tsx`. Both
pages' "Try again" fetch the page again.

---

## 5. Common page rules and layouts

### 5.1 How to read sections 5 and 6

Sections 5 and 6 are the work order for `GET /_page` and the first document:
what the Rust server computes for each layout and page of upstream's Next.js
16.2.7 App Router so the SPA renders 1:1. Citations:

- bare paths (`lib/config.ts:217`) are upstream: `upstream/Modlight/` (the specification);
- `port:` paths are the SPA: `server/web/src/` (e.g. `port:pages/(auth)/login/page.tsx:13`);
- `next:` paths are Next 16.2.7's `dist/` (the `next` package of upstream's lock file);
- "reference" is the original server at http://127.0.0.1:8420, fetched anonymously
  with `curl -H 'Accept-Language: en'` (no sign-in, GET only). Everything marked
  **verified** was checked there on 2026-10-18. Signed-in pages and found media
  could not be checked that way; their heads and data follow from the upstream code.

Conventions used throughout:

- **`first(v)`** = `Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v`. Next's `searchParams` object maps a
  query key to a string, or to a string array when the key repeats (URLSearchParams
  semantics: percent-decoding, `+` is a space). `first()` therefore takes the FIRST
  occurrence.
- **`Number(s)`** is JS ToNumber: surrounding white space is ignored, `""` and
  white space only give `0`, decimal / exponent (`1e2`) / `Infinity` / `0x10`, `0o7`,
  `0b1` are accepted, anything else (including `"1px"`) is `NaN`, `Number(undefined)`
  is `NaN`.
- **`a || b`**: `b` when `a` is falsy (`""`, `0`, `NaN`, `null`, `undefined`, `false`).
  **`a ?? b`**: `b` only when `a` is `null`/`undefined`.
- **`.trim()`** is JS trim: Unicode `White_Space` plus line terminators and U+FEFF.
- **Dates.** A prop typed `Date` in the port travels as `{"$date": "<toISOString()>"}`.
  A prop typed `string` that upstream produced with `.toISOString()` travels as a
  plain string `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss.sssZ` (UTC, milliseconds). Postgres `bigint`
  columns declared `mode: "number"` are plain JSON numbers.
- **Optional props** (`field?: T`): omit the key (or send `{"$undefined":1}`) where
  upstream had `undefined`; send `null` only where upstream had `null`. The port
  distinguishes the two in several places (e.g. dashboard `folderId`).
- **Redirects**: Next `redirect(url)` from a page or layout is HTTP **307**
  (`NEXT_REDIRECT;replace;<url>;307;`), `permanentRedirect` 308. The payload is
  `{"redirect": url, "status": 307}`.
- **Secrets**: build every prop from the columns listed for it; the "Never sent"
  lines name what upstream deliberately keeps on the server. Never serialise whole
  rows.

### 5.2 Request pipeline (document load and `/_page` alike)

Evaluate in this order. `/_page` decodes its first `path` parameter once, which gives
the browser's (still percent-encoded) pathname, and applies every step to it as if it
were the request path (the Host header is the `/_page` request's own). The page's own
query (`searchParams`) is every pair of the `/_page` query string EXCEPT that first
`path` pair, in order (section 4.1): a page URL may legitimately carry its
own `path` key, and `/dashboard` (section 6.11) tests whether the page query has any
key at all.

1. **Trailing slash** (Next default): a path ending in `/` other than `/` itself is
   answered **308** to the same path without the slash, query kept
   (**verified**: `/dashboard/` -> `308 Location: /dashboard`,
   `/login/?next=%2Fx` -> `308 Location: /login?next=%2Fx`).
2. **`proxy.ts` shortener rewrite** (`proxy.ts:21-52`): when `SHORTLINK_URL` is set
   and parses, `SHORT_HOST` = its hostname lowercased. If the request `Host` header
   (text before the first `:`, lowercased) equals `SHORT_HOST`, the pathname is not
   `/`, and the pathname does NOT match
   `/^\/(?:_next|api|s\/|favicon|icon|apple-icon|robots|sitemap|\.well-known)/`,
   the request is internally rewritten to `/s` + pathname (so `s.example.com/promo/unlock`
   is the page `/s/[code]/unlock` with `code=promo`). The browser URL is unchanged.
3. **`proxy.ts` optimistic dashboard gate** (`proxy.ts:54-61`): if
   `pathname.startsWith("/dashboard")` (case-sensitive, so `/dashboardx` too) and the
   request carries no session cookie, answer **307** `Location: /login?next=<pathname>`
   where `<pathname>` is the RAW (still percent-encoded) pathname serialised with
   URLSearchParams rules; the query string is dropped. Session cookie present =
   a non-empty value for `better-auth.session_token`, `__Secure-better-auth.session_token`,
   `better-auth-session_token` or `__Secure-better-auth-session_token` (Better Auth
   1.6.13 `getSessionCookie`, `web/node_modules/better-auth/dist/cookies/index.mjs:197-206`;
   validity is NOT checked here). **Verified**: no cookie -> `/login?next=%2Fdashboard`;
   `/dashboard/a%20b` -> `/login?next=%2Fdashboard%2Fa%2520b`;
   `/dashboard/%C3%B3?x=1` -> `/login?next=%2Fdashboard%2F%25C3%25B3`;
   `/dashboardx` -> `/login?next=%2Fdashboardx`; `better-auth.session_token=`
   (empty) -> gate fires; `better-auth.session_token=garbage` or
   `__Secure-better-auth.session_token=garbage` -> gate passes and the `(app)` layout's
   `requireUser()` answers `307 Location: /login` (no `next`).
4. **Route matching** (section 5.3), then **layout data** (root, then the group
   layout) and **page data**. Upstream renders the layouts, the page and
   `generateMetadata` concurrently; every page below the `(app)` layout re-checks the
   session itself, so evaluating the layout gate first and the page afterwards gives
   the same outcome. Where a page has GET side effects this is noted in its section.

### 5.3 Route table and precedence

Next matches per segment, static before dynamic; route groups `(app)`/`(auth)` add no
segment. Pages (route id = the Next app path with route groups stripped, as in
`port:contract/routes.ts:7-90`; the upstream file is `app/<group>/<path>/page.tsx`):

| URL pattern | Route id | Layouts |
|---|---|---|
| `/` | `/` | root |
| `/welcome` | `/welcome` | root |
| `/download` | `/download` | root |
| `/dashboard` and 9 static children, `/dashboard/admin/users/:id/files` | `/dashboard...` | root, (app) |
| `/2fa` `/authorize` `/forgot` `/login` `/register` `/reset` `/invite/:token` | `/2fa` ... `/invite/[token]` | root, (auth) |
| `/:userCode` | `/[userCode]` | root |
| `/:userCode/:mediaCode` | `/[userCode]/[mediaCode]` | root |
| `/a/:userCode/:slug` | `/a/[userCode]/[slug]` | root |
| `/s/:code/unlock` | `/s/[code]/unlock` | root |
| `/s/:code/:name/unlock` | `/s/[code]/[name]/unlock` | root |
| `/u/:userCode` | `/u/[userCode]` | root |

Route handlers share the same URL space and are NOT pages (`/_page` answers
`{"reload": true}`): the 25 `handler(...)` rows of `server/src/pages/table.rs`
(`/api/auth/[...all]` and 14 static `/api/...` paths, `/oembed`,
`/embed/:version/:card`, `/avatar/:userCode`, `/bg/:userCode`, `/download/app`,
`/s/:code`, `/s/:code/:name`, `/a/:userCode/:slug/zip`,
`/:userCode/:mediaCode/:action`, `/:userCode/:mediaCode/delete/:token`), the tus
endpoint `/api/files*` of upstream `server.ts`, and static files. Any other
`/api/x` is a page like any two-segment path (`/api/foo` is
`/[userCode]/[mediaCode]`), and so are GET `/upload` and GET `/api/upload`
(`server.ts` takes only their POSTs). The SPA keeps the same list
(`port:contract/routes.ts` `SERVER_PATHS`; `tests/route-table.test.ts` compares
it, and the server's own matching tests, with `table.rs`) and loads those URLs
as documents without asking `/_page`.

Consequences (**verified**): `/download/x`, `/login/x`, `/welcome/x`, `/a/x`,
`/dashboard/x` (with a cookie) are `/[userCode]/[mediaCode]` (404 "Not found");
`/u`, `/a`, `/s` are `/[userCode]` (404); `/dashboard/admin/x` is the route handler
`/[userCode]/[mediaCode]/[action]`; `/dashboard/admin/users/x`, `/x/y/z/w`,
`/s/x/unlock/y` match nothing (the not-found page, section 5.11). `/Dashboard` is
`/[userCode]` (matching and the gate are case-sensitive).

**Params.** Next decodes each matched segment once (`decodeURIComponent`). Then, in
16.2.7, the two consumers see DIFFERENT spellings:

- the page (and layout) component receives `encodeURIComponent(decoded)`
  (`next:shared/lib/router/utils/get-dynamic-param.js:48-60,107-149` "the value that
  is passed to user code", used by `next:server/app-render/create-component-tree.js:290-298`):
  `/u/a%20b` -> `"a%20b"`, `/u/%C3%B3` -> `"%C3%B3"`, `/x/a@b` -> `"a%40b"`,
  `/x/f.png` -> `"f.png"`;
- `generateMetadata` receives the decoded string (`interpolatedParams`,
  `next:lib/metadata/resolve-metadata.js:496-505`): `"a b"`, `"ó"`, `"a@b"`.

**Verified** on the reference with `/x%25`: the page saw `"x%25"` (its second
`decodeURIComponent` worked and it answered notFound) while `generateMetadata` saw
`"x%"` and threw `URIError` (RSC row `E{"digest":"2215873025"}` in the metadata
outlet), status 404. Every route section below states which spelling each value
uses inside the computation. The payload's `params` carries the DECODED values, as
section 4.2 says: no SPA code reads them, and no upstream client
component calls `useParams()` (which would have seen the encoded spelling). A
malformed escape in the URL itself (`/u/%E0%A4%A`, `/invite/%E0%A4%A`) is
answered with `500 text/plain "Internal Server Error"` (no page, no payload;
verified). The skeleton answers `400` `{"message": "Bad Request"}` instead, on
the document and on `/_page`; the SPA loads any `/_page` answer that is not a
page payload as a document (section 4.2), so either works (open question 12.5).

### 5.4 Payload shapes and statuses

The envelope is section 4.2 (`port:runtime/payload.ts`): a page answers
`{route, params, layouts, props, meta}`; `redirect()` `{redirect, status}`;
`notFound()` and unmatched URLs `{notFound: true, layouts: {root}, meta}`; route
handlers and files `{reload: true}`; a failed loader `{error: {message, digest?},
layouts: <those that loaded>}`. Document statuses: 200 page, 307 + `Location`
redirect, 404 not found, 500 error, except inside the dashboard boundary (section
5.11). What this document adds to that envelope:

- `route` is the id of `port:contract/routes.ts` (route groups stripped:
  `"/dashboard/folders"`, `"/login"`, `"/[userCode]/[mediaCode]"`).
- `layouts.root` always; `layouts["(app)"]` for every `/dashboard...` route;
  `layouts["(auth)"]` for `/2fa`, `/authorize`, `/forgot`, `/invite/[token]`,
  `/login`, `/register`, `/reset`, and it **must be the empty object `{}`**: the SPA
  loads that layout only when the value is truthy (`port:routes/loader.ts:100-101`).
- `params`: the dynamic segments by name, decoded (`{}` for static routes), e.g.
  `{"userCode":"ab","mediaCode":"x1.png"}`. No port reads it
  (`port:routes/segments.tsx` only forwards it).
- `error.message`: upstream production builds mask every server error to the text in
  `port:runtime/errors.ts:23-24`; `digest` is an opaque id (upstream: a hash string,
  e.g. reference `"1734733684"`). A failure in a `/dashboard...` page after the
  `(app)` layout loaded keeps `layouts["(app)"]`, so `app/(app)/dashboard/error.tsx`
  renders inside the panel.

### 5.5 Session helpers (`lib/auth-helpers.ts`)

- `getSession()` (`:7-9`): Better Auth session lookup from the request cookies; `null`
  when signed out. Upstream (Better Auth 1.6.13, no session options in `lib/auth.ts`)
  reads the `session` row by cookie token, treats a missing or expired row as signed
  out (deleting the expired row and clearing the cookie), and returns the live `user`
  row (no cookie cache); refreshing `expires_at` is the auth module's business
  (PORT.md section 9; section 4.1: no refresh on `/_page`).
- `requireUser()` (`:12-16`): no session -> `redirect("/login")` (bare, no `next`).
- `requireAdmin()` (`:19-23`): `requireUser()`, then `session.user.role !== "admin"`
  -> `redirect("/dashboard")`.
- `session.user` columns used by pages: `id`, `name`, `email`, `image`, `role`,
  `userCode`, `maxQuota` (bigint, number, default -1), `theme` (text, default
  "discord"), `copyRaw` (bool) (`lib/auth.ts:179-189`, `lib/db/schema/auth-schema.ts`).

### 5.6 Cookies (as Next reads them)

Next's `cookies()` (`next:compiled/@edge-runtime/cookies/index.js:48-65`, behaviour
verified on the reference): the `Cookie` header is split on `/; */` (a semicolon and
any following spaces); a pair without `=` gets the value `"true"`; values are
`decodeURIComponent`-decoded, and a pair whose value fails to decode is dropped
(`ml_mode=%E0%A4%A` is "absent"); values are NOT trimmed (`ml_mode=dark ` is not
`dark`); the name is not trimmed on the right (`ml_mode =dark` is a different name);
when a name repeats the LAST occurrence wins (`ml_mode=dark; ml_mode=light` -> light).

### 5.7 Root layout -> `layouts.root` (`app/layout.tsx`, `port:contract/layouts.ts:32-60`)

| Field | Type | Upstream source |
|---|---|---|
| `locale` | `"en" \| "pl"` | `i18n/request.ts:22-30` (getLocale) |
| `timeZone` | `string` | next-intl default: `Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone` of the server process (`next-intl@4.13.0 dist/esm/development/server/react-server/getConfig.js:10-12,54`); `i18n/request.ts` sets none. Reference: `"UTC"` (from its RSC payload) |
| `mode` | `"light" \| "dark" \| "system"` | `resolveMode(cookie ml_mode)` `app/layout.tsx:41`, `lib/theme-cookies.ts:18-20` |
| `theme` | ThemeId | `resolveTheme(session?.user.theme ?? cookie ml_skin)` `app/layout.tsx:59`, `lib/themes.ts:114-118` (the brief calls it `skin`) |
| `messages` | object \| null | the 17 namespaces `common nav auth dashboard profile paste links albums tokens admin public landing viewer files upload download authorize`, plus `turnstile` (not upstream: section 11), of `messages/<locale>/<ns>.json`, keyed by namespace (`i18n/request.ts:9-14,32-37`, handed to `NextIntlClientProvider` at `app/layout.tsx:29,70`); `null` when the request's `X-Modlight-Messages: <locale>:<messagesHash>` matches (section 4.1) |
| `messagesHash` | string | fingerprint of `messages` (server-defined, `port:runtime/messages.ts:21-23`) |
| `config` | `PublicConfig` | `lib/config.ts:216-250`, five fields below, plus `turnstileSiteKey` (not upstream); optional in the type, always send it |

**locale** (`i18n/request.ts:24-30`): `c = cookie ml_locale`. If `c` is `"pl"` or `"en"`
-> that. If `c` is any other NON-EMPTY string -> `"en"` and Accept-Language is NOT
consulted. If `c` is absent or `""` -> `"pl"` when the `Accept-Language` header
(missing = `""`) matches `/(?:^|[,\s])pl\b/i` (JS `\s`, JS `\b`: `pl` followed by a
non-word char or the end; quality values are ignored), else `"en"`. **Verified**:
`pl-PL,pl;q=0.9` -> pl, `en-US,pl;q=0.1` -> pl, `de, PL` -> pl, `xpl` -> en,
`ml_locale=de` + `pl` -> en, `ml_locale=` + `pl` -> pl, `ml_locale=pl` -> pl.

**mode**: exactly `light`, `dark` or `system` (case-sensitive) else `"system"`
(**verified**: `DARK` -> system, `"dark"` with quotes -> system).

**theme**: `session` is `getSession()` with failures mapped to `null`
(`app/layout.tsx:33`). Input = `session.user.theme` unless that is `null`/`undefined`
(signed out, or a NULL column), then cookie `ml_skin`. `resolveTheme(v)`: `v` is one of
the 14 ids `default glass synthwave terminal paper win98 nebula grimoire cartoon mono
deco riso discord spotify` (`lib/themes.ts:8-72`, case-sensitive) -> `v`; `v === "modern"`
-> `"default"` (`lib/themes.ts:100-102`); anything else, `""` included -> `"discord"`
(`lib/themes.ts:86`). **Verified**: `ml_skin=paper` -> paper, `modern` -> default,
`%70aper` -> paper. Quirk: the alias test is `v in THEME_ALIASES`, which is true for
`Object.prototype` names; `ml_skin=toString|constructor|__proto__` makes the
reference answer **500** with the error shell (verified). See open question 12.8.

**config** (`PublicConfig`, `port:contract/layouts.ts:15-26`; never send any other
config field):

| Field | Source |
|---|---|
| `appName` | `process.env.APP_NAME?.trim() \|\| "Modlight"` (`lib/config.ts:217`) |
| `embedBrand` | `EMBED_BRAND?.trim() \|\| brandFromUrl(APP_URL) \|\| APP_NAME?.trim() \|\| "Modlight"` (`:230-234`; `brandFromUrl` `:173-197`) |
| `appUrl` | `normalizeOrigin(APP_URL?.trim() \|\| "http://localhost:8420")`, trailing slashes stripped (`:137-141`) |
| `appDomains` | `[appUrl, ...APP_DOMAINS.split(",").map(normalizeOrigin).filter(Boolean)]`, deduplicated case-insensitively keeping the first spelling (`:199-214`) |
| `shortUrl` | `normalizeOrigin(SHORTLINK_URL?.trim() \|\| appUrl)` (`:250`) |
| `turnstileSiteKey` | Not upstream: the Cloudflare Turnstile site key while the optional check is on (`server/src/turnstile.rs`: panel block, else TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY + TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY), else `null`. The SPA renders the widget only when it is a string (section 11). |

Nothing else in the root layout reads data (`app/layout.tsx:27-34`: locale,
messages, cookies, session).

### 5.8 `(app)` layout -> `layouts["(app)"]` (`app/(app)/layout.tsx`, `port:contract/layouts.ts:63-83`)

Gate: `requireUser()` (`app/(app)/layout.tsx:40`) -> `{redirect:"/login",status:307}`
(document: 307, verified with a garbage cookie on `/dashboard/admin`). Then, for
`uid = session.user.id`, four reads (`:42-50`): `tree = folderTree(uid)`,
`counts = kindCounts(uid)`, `stats = userStats(uid)`, `prefs = getUserPrefs(uid)`
(may be `null`).

| Field | Type | Source and shaping |
|---|---|---|
| `theme` | `string \| null` | `session.user.theme`, raw (`:67`) |
| `background` | `{url: string; dim: number} \| null` | `backgroundStyle(prefs?.userCode, prefs?.backgroundKey, prefs?.backgroundDim)` (`:68`, `lib/background.ts:115-123`) |
| `transferFolders` | `{id: string; name: string}[]` | `flattenFolders(tree)` (`:31-37,76`): pre-order depth-first walk of `tree` |
| `defaultFolderId` | `string \| null` | `prefs?.defaultFolderId ?? null` (`:77`) |
| `user` | `ShellUser` | `{name: session.user.name, email: session.user.email, image: avatarUrl(prefs?.userCode ?? session.user.userCode, session.user.image), role: session.user.role, userCode: session.user.userCode}` (`:80-89`); type `components/app-shell.tsx:72-85` (`name?`, `image?`, `role?`, `userCode?` are `string \| null`) |
| `sidebar` | `ShellSidebarData` | `{folders: toTreeNodes(tree), counts, totalFiles: stats.count, unsortedCount: Math.max(0, stats.count - inFolders), storage: {used: stats.totalSize, max: session.user.maxQuota ?? -1}}` (`:54-62`); type `components/app-shell.tsx:91-98` |
| `defaultCollapsed` | `boolean` | cookie `ml_sidebar` `=== "collapsed"` (`:91`) |
| `duplicateReminder` | `{redundant: number; reclaimable: number} \| null` | DuplicateReminderSlot decision, below |

Definitions:

- **`listUserFolders(uid)`** (`lib/data/folders.ts:44-87`): all `folders` rows with
  `owner_id = uid`, ordered `sort_order ASC, name ASC` (database collation), each
  `{id, parentId, name, slug, isPublic, sortOrder, createdAt, count, size}` where
  `count`/`size` = `count(*)` / `sum(size)` of `uploads` with `owner_id = uid AND
  deleted_at IS NULL AND folder_id = folders.id` (0 when none; `size` read as text
  then `Number`).
- **`buildFolderTree(rows)`** (`lib/data/folders.ts:141-172`): a node is a ROOT when
  `parentId` is null, or names no row of this user, or equals its own id; otherwise it
  is appended to its parent's `children`. Both roots and children keep the row order
  above. `totalCount`/`totalSize` = own `count`/`size` plus all descendants
  (depth-first, a per-root `seen` set stops cycles). Nodes caught in a parent cycle of
  length >= 2 are reachable from no root and are therefore absent from the tree (and
  from everything derived from it).
- **`toTreeNodes`** (`app/(app)/layout.tsx:17-28`): each node becomes
  `{id, parentId, name, slug, isPublic, count, totalCount, children}` (no `sortOrder`,
  `createdAt`, `size`, `totalSize`); type `FolderTreeNode`
  (`components/dashboard/FolderTree.tsx:245-256`).
- **`inFolders`** = sum of `totalCount` over the ROOT nodes (`:54`).
- **`kindCounts(uid)`** (`lib/data/uploads.ts:268-283`): object with all seven keys in
  this order, initialised to 0: `image video audio document archive paste other`;
  rows `SELECT mime_type, kind, count(*) FROM uploads WHERE owner_id = uid AND
  deleted_at IS NULL GROUP BY mime_type, kind`, each added to
  `kindOf(mime_type, kind)` with NO extension (`lib/data/uploads.ts:53-66`):
  `kind === "paste"` -> paste; else `m = effectiveType(mime)` (`lib/media/mime.ts:46-53`:
  `mime.split(";")[0].trim().toLowerCase()`; if empty or one of the "useless" types
  `"" */* application/octet-stream binary/octet-stream application/octetstream
  application/x-download application/download application/force-download
  application/unknown unknown/unknown` (`lib/media/sniff.ts:189-200`) the extension
  lookup runs, which with no extension yields nothing, so `m` stays as is); then
  `image/`* -> image, `video/`* -> video, `audio/`* -> audio, `text/`* or one of the 7
  DOCUMENT_MIMES -> document, one of the 6 ARCHIVE_MIMES -> archive, else other
  (lists at `lib/data/uploads.ts:33-50`).
- **`userStats(uid)`** (`lib/data/uploads.ts:251-265`): `count(*)`,
  `coalesce(sum(size),0)` over `uploads WHERE owner_id = uid AND deleted_at IS NULL`,
  both `Number`.
- **`getUserPrefs(uid)`** (`lib/data/user-prefs.ts:39-63`): the `user` row's
  `user_code, default_folder_id, background_key, background_dim` (+ others not used
  here); `backgroundDim ?? 70`. `null` when the row is missing.
- **`backgroundStyle(userCode, key, dim)`** (`lib/background.ts:99-123`): `null` unless
  both `userCode` and `key` are non-empty. `token` = text after the last `/` of `key`,
  minus a trailing `.webp`, then the text after its last `-` (`""` when there is no
  `-`). `url = "/bg/" + encodeURIComponent(userCode) + (token ? "?v=" +
  encodeURIComponent(token) : "")`. `dim = clampDim(dim ?? 70) / 100` with
  `clampDim`: `null`/`undefined`/`""` -> 70; non-finite -> 70; else
  `min(95, max(0, Math.round(n)))` (JS rounding, .5 up). JSON number, e.g. `0.7`.
- **`avatarUrl(userCode, image)`** (`lib/avatar.ts:130-140`): `null` when `image` is
  empty; `image` itself when it matches `/^https?:\/\//i` or starts with `/`; `null`
  when `userCode` is empty or `image` does not start with `"avatars/"`; else
  `"/avatar/" + encodeURIComponent(userCode) + (token ? "?v=" + encodeURIComponent(token) : "")`,
  token derived from `image` exactly as for the wallpaper. Never send the storage key.
- **DuplicateReminderSlot** (`components/duplicates/DuplicateReminderSlot.tsx:31-47`,
  port `port:overrides/components/duplicates/DuplicateReminderSlot.tsx`), in this order:
  1. cookie `ml_dupes_snooze` (`components/duplicates/snooze.ts:22,41-45`): when it is
     non-empty and `Number(value)` is finite and `> Date.now()` (epoch ms) ->
     `null`, **no query**;
  2. `duplicateReminder(uid)` (`lib/data/duplicates.ts:492-503`), one SQL statement
     (`:468-485` with the CTE `:340-356`):
     ```sql
     with dupes as (
       select checksum, owner_id, count(*)::int as copies,
              sum(size)::bigint as bytes, max(size)::bigint as keep
       from uploads
       where owner_id = $uid and deleted_at is null
         and checksum is not null and length(checksum) > 0
       group by checksum, owner_id
       having count(*) > 1)
     select count(*)::int as groups,
            coalesce(sum(d.copies - 1), 0)::int as redundant,
            coalesce(sum(d.bytes - d.keep), 0)::bigint as reclaimable
     from dupes d
     where not exists (select 1 from duplicate_dismissals
                       where owner_id = d.owner_id and checksum = d.checksum)
     ```
     values through `Number`, non-finite -> 0 (`:268-271`);
  3. due = `redundant > 10 || reclaimable > 1073741824` (`:442-453`); not due -> `null`;
     due -> `{redundant, reclaimable}`;
  4. any error in step 2 -> `null` (logged, never fails the layout).

Any other failure in this layout is an error payload with `layouts: {root}`.

Pre-paint side of this layout (HTML document only, section 5.12): the panel's
`<ThemeApplier theme={session.user.theme} background={...}>` (`:66-69`) also
persists the skin: its inline script writes the cookie
`ml_skin=<resolveTheme(session.user.theme)>;path=/;max-age=31536000;samesite=lax`
(`components/theme-applier.tsx:61-62,87`, persist is true on every `/dashboard...`
path). In the SPA that write is done client-side by the same component; the server
sets no cookie.

### 5.9 `(auth)` layout -> `layouts["(auth)"] = {}`

`app/(auth)/layout.tsx` has no gate and reads no data: it renders `config.appName`
(root `config`), the `auth.layout.*` messages and `new Date().getFullYear()` (computed
in the browser by the port). Send `{}` (section 5.4).

### 5.10 Metadata -> `meta` and the document head

**Root metadata** (`app/layout.tsx:18-22`), inherited by every page:

```ts
{ title: { default: A, template: `%s - ${A}` },
  description: D,
  applicationName: A }
```

`A` = `config.appName`; `D` = the fixed English string
`Fast, modern, self-hosted file & screenshot sharing with rock-solid large-file uploads and first-class ShareX support.`
(not translated). No layout below the root exports metadata; no `metadataBase` is
set anywhere.

**Merging** (`next:lib/metadata/resolve-metadata.js:166-312` mergeMetadata, `:745-811` accumulateMetadata, titles
`next:lib/metadata/resolvers/resolve-title.js`):

- A page `title` that is a string becomes `template.replace(/%s/g, title)` =
  `"<title> - A"`. `{absolute: t}` is used as is. No page title -> the root default
  `A`, NOT templated.
- Every other key replaces the inherited value as a whole (a page `description`
  replaces `D`; `robots`, `openGraph`, `twitter`, `alternates`, `other` are only ever
  set by pages). An explicit `null` clears the inherited value (`resolve-metadata.js`
  `metadata[key] ?? null`): only the media page (6.27) sets `description: null` and
  `applicationName: null`; everywhere else `applicationName` is `A`.
- `robots: {index: false, follow: false}` renders `"noindex, nofollow"`
  (`next:lib/metadata/resolvers/resolve-basics.js:138-160`: `index`/`noindex`, then
  `follow`/`nofollow`, then any of `noarchive nosnippet noimageindex nocache
  notranslate indexifembedded nositelinkssearchbox unavailable_after
  max-video-preview max-image-preview max-snippet` in that order, joined by `", "`).
- **OpenGraph -> Twitter autofill** (`resolve-metadata.js:603-668` inheritFromMetadata/postProcessMetadata): when a page sets
  `openGraph`, Twitter values the page did not set are filled from it: `title` from
  `openGraph.title` (else the page title), `description` from
  `openGraph.description || description`, `images` from `openGraph.images`; the card
  defaults to `summary_large_image` when there are images, else `summary`
  (`resolve-opengraph.js:163-197`). Then an `openGraph`/`twitter` block without a
  title or description inherits the page's resolved `title`/`description`. So a page
  that sets only `openGraph` still emits `twitter:*` tags.
- OG/Twitter **image** URLs are parsed with WHATWG `new URL()` and re-serialised
  (`resolve-opengraph.js:63-120`, `resolve-url.js:70-86`): absolute URLs come back
  normalised (lower-case scheme/host, default port dropped, an origin-only URL gains a
  trailing `/`, unsafe characters percent-encoded); a RELATIVE image URL is resolved
  against `http://localhost:${PORT || 3000}` because no `metadataBase` exists. `og:url`,
  `alternates.*`, `og:video`, `og:audio` and `twitter:player*` values are NOT
  normalised (strings pass through; `./`-relative ones are resolved against the
  pathname). The Rust side should use the `url` crate (WHATWG) for the normalised ones.
- No `openGraph`/`twitter` title template exists (the root sets none), so `og:title`
  and `twitter:title` are never templated by the root.

**Tag order** Next emits (`next:lib/metadata/metadata.js:276-1685`), skipping absent
values: `<title>`; `description`; `application-name`; author links/metas; manifest;
`generator`; `keywords`; `referrer`; `creator`; `publisher`; `robots`; `googlebot`;
`abstract`; archives/assets/bookmarks; prev/next; `category`; `classification`;
`other` entries (`<meta name=K content=V>`, arrays expanded, empty values skipped);
alternates (`canonical`, then `alternate` + `hrefLang`, + `media`, + `type`, each with
`title` when given); `apple-itunes-app`; `fb:*`; pinterest; `format-detection`;
verification; apple web app; **Open Graph** (`og:determiner`, `og:title`,
`og:description`, `og:url`, `og:site_name`, `og:locale`, `og:country_name`, `og:ttl`,
then per image `og:image`, `og:image:secure_url`, `og:image:type`, `og:image:width`,
`og:image:height`, `og:image:alt`, per video `og:video`, `og:video:secure_url`,
`og:video:type`, `og:video:width`, `og:video:height`, per audio `og:audio`,
`og:audio:secure_url`, `og:audio:type`, then emails/phones/faxes/`og:locale:alternate`,
and **`og:type` last** followed by its type-specific tags); **Twitter**
(`twitter:card`, `twitter:site`, `twitter:site:id`, `twitter:creator`,
`twitter:creator:id`, `twitter:title`, `twitter:description`, per image
`twitter:image`, `twitter:image:alt`, `twitter:image:secure_url`, `twitter:image:type`,
`twitter:image:width`, `twitter:image:height`; for `card: "player"` per player
`twitter:player`, `twitter:player:stream`, `twitter:player:width`,
`twitter:player:height`); `al:*` app links; **icons** last. OG tags use the
`property` attribute, Twitter tags the `name` attribute. Width/height/alt tags are
emitted only when truthy (`0` and `""` are skipped).

**Icons**: Next appends the two file-based icons (`app/icon.svg`, `app/apple-icon.png`)
to every page, last: `<link rel="icon" href="/icon.svg?<build hash>" sizes="any"
type="image/svg+xml">` and `<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/apple-icon.png?<hash>"
sizes="180x180" type="image/png">` (verified on every page, e.g.
`/icon.svg?icon.39ljpx4vz_l6l.svg`). The Rust head serves them as `/icon.svg` and
`/apple-icon.png` (section 2.2: the template has no icon links).

**Payload and document.** Two consumers, one resolution:

- `meta` in the payload (section 4.2, `server/src/pages/meta.rs`
  `ResolvedHead`) = `{title, description, applicationName, robots}`: the `<title>`
  text and the `content` of the `description`, `application-name` and `robots` tags
  of the list, `null` where the tag is absent. (The brief's `{title, tags}` shape is
  that same list as `{"tag": "meta"|"link", "attrs": {...}}` objects, attribute names
  as in HTML: `name`, `property`, `content`, `rel`, `href`, `type`, `title`,
  `hreflang`, `media`.)
- The first document's `<head>` carries the whole list in Next's order, icons
  included; Open Graph, Twitter and alternate tags exist only there (link-preview
  bots read nothing else). Values are plain text; the renderer escapes them as React
  does (`&`, `<`, `>`, `"`, `'`).

Each route section gives the upstream metadata object and, where it is more than
`Std`, the ordered tag list; `meta` is read off that list.

Below, `Std` = `[meta description=D, meta application-name=A]`, and `{title: X, tags: Std}`
is shorthand for `meta = {title: X, description: D, applicationName: A, robots: null}`
with the head `<title>X</title>`, the two `Std` tags and the icons.

### 5.11 Not-found, redirect and error rules

| Case | Upstream status | Upstream HTML head | Browser after JS or client navigation (head, UI) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Unmatched URL** (no page, no handler) | 404 | normal document: `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` first, then `<title>A</title>` + `Std` (verified `/x/y/z/w`) | same |
| **`notFound()` from a page outside the dashboard boundary** (the public pages; no `(auth)` page calls it) | 404 | **error shell** `<html id="__next_error__">` with NO `lang`/`data-*` attributes, empty body; head = `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` + root metadata only (`<title>A</title>` + `Std`) (verified `/u/nonexistent`, `/x`, `/x/y`, `/a/x/y`, `/s/nonexistent/unlock`, `/s/nonexistent/foo/unlock`) | the page's own `generateMetadata` result merged with the root (e.g. `Not found - A` + robots `noindex, nofollow`), WITHOUT the 404 robots tag; the not-found page is rendered inside the root layout by the client (`next:server/app-render/app-render.js:1959-2058`: error shell + main RSC data) |
| **`notFound()` from a page inside `app/(app)/dashboard/loading.tsx`'s Suspense boundary** (every `/dashboard...` page) | **200** (shell already streamed) | normal document with the loading skeleton; `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` inserted (`next:server/app-render/make-get-server-inserted-html.js:44-51`) | page metadata; the ROOT not-found page replaces the whole panel (no not-found file exists below the root) |
| **`redirect()` from a page inside that boundary** (`/dashboard` -> `/dashboard/folders`, `requireAdmin` -> `/dashboard`, ...) | **200** | skeleton + `<meta id="__next-page-redirect" http-equiv="refresh" content="1;url=<target>">` (`make-get-server-inserted-html.js:52-62`), then a client-side replace | n/a |
| `redirect()` anywhere else (root page, `(app)` layout `requireUser`, `/authorize`) | 307 + `Location` (verified) | n/a | n/a |
| Uncaught error outside the dashboard boundary | 500 | error shell, head = robots `noindex` + the page's metadata (verified with `ml_skin=toString`) | UI: root `app/error.tsx` (`GlobalRouteError`) |
| Uncaught error inside the dashboard boundary | 200 | normal document with the skeleton | UI: `app/(app)/dashboard/error.tsx` inside the panel |

Pages that render the not-found page, and their status:

| Route | notFound when | Status |
|---|---|---|
| unmatched URL | no page and no route handler matches | 404 |
| `/[userCode]/[mediaCode]` (6.27) | no live file for tag + code (and no tagless-action redirect); unpublished for a non-privileged viewer; view budget refused | 404 |
| `/[userCode]` (6.26) | segment is not a tagless code of an opted-in owner, then as 6.27 | 404 |
| `/u/[userCode]` (6.22) | no user with that tag, or `public_profile` not true | 404 |
| `/a/[userCode]/[slug]` (6.23) | no owner by tag or alias, no folder with that slug, or folder not public | 404 |
| `/s/[code]/unlock`, `/s/[code]/[name]/unlock` (6.24, 6.25) | no short link with that code | 404 |
| `/dashboard/folders` (6.12) | `?folder=` names no reachable folder of the viewer | 200 (inside the boundary) |
| `/dashboard/admin/users/[id]/files` (6.21) | no such user | 200 (inside the boundary) |

No `(auth)` page, no other dashboard page and none of `/`, `/welcome`, `/download`
ever calls `notFound()`.

Contract for the Rust server (statuses and documents: section 2.1, which
also records the dashboard behaviour as seen on the reference with a session: a
non-admin on `/dashboard/admin` gets 200 and lands on `/dashboard`;
`/dashboard/admin/users/<unknown>/files` gets 200 with the page title plus robots
`noindex`):

- **`notFound` payload `meta`** = what upstream's SERVER renders in the head:
  - outside the dashboard boundary (unmatched URLs and every public page):
    `{title: A, description: D, applicationName: A, robots: "noindex"}`; document
    head `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">`, `<title>A</title>`, description,
    application-name, icons (**verified** on all six 404 pages and on `/x/y/z/w`,
    identical for a Discordbot user agent);
  - inside the dashboard boundary: the page's own metadata (per route) with
    `robots: "noindex"` (`/dashboard/folders`:
    `{title: A, description: D, applicationName: A, robots: "noindex"}`;
    `/dashboard/admin/users/[id]/files`: `User files - A` instead of `A`).

  Upstream's browser later swaps the head of a public not-found page for the page's
  own `generateMetadata` result (e.g. `Not found - A` + robots `noindex, nofollow`),
  because the inlined RSC data carries it. Do not reproduce that swap: for an
  unpublished file viewed by a stranger that result is the `unlisted` variant whose
  title is the file NAME (section 6.27 F2), so upstream leaks it in the page source
  and the tab title. open question 12.7.
- The not-found UI is `app/not-found.tsx` (port `port:pages/not-found.tsx`); it needs
  no data. Documents of a `notFound` payload use the normal shell with the root
  `<html>` attributes (upstream's attribute-less error shell is a streaming artefact
  that the client replaces at once).

### 5.12 HTML document

Shell mechanics are section 2.2; the upstream facts it must reproduce:

- `<html lang="{root.locale}" data-mode="{root.mode}" data-theme="{root.theme}"
  class="<GeistSans var> <GeistMono var> h-full antialiased">` and
  `<body class="bg-background text-foreground min-h-full">` (`app/layout.tsx:62-69`;
  the SPA template carries the class lists, `port:../index.html:2,9`). `data-mode`
  carries the COOKIE mode (`system` included); upstream then runs next-themes'
  blocking script at the start of `<body>` (verified; the SPA template has the same
  script reading `data-mode`):
  `((a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h)=>{...})("data-mode","theme",<mode>,null,["light","dark"],null,true,true)`,
  which sets `data-mode` to `localStorage.theme || <mode>` resolved through
  `prefers-color-scheme` when `system`, and `style.colorScheme`.
- **`data-theme` and wallpaper before first paint.** Upstream's `<ThemeApplier>`
  emits a blocking inline script (`components/theme-applier.tsx:78-88`) that runs
  before the first paint and sets on `<html>`:
  `data-theme = resolveTheme(theme)`; when `background` is non-null
  `style` properties `--ml-bg-image: url("<background.url>")` and
  `--ml-bg-dim: String(background.dim)` plus `data-bg="on"`; when null it removes
  all three. The Rust shell must therefore render, for every page whose payload
  contains a ThemeApplier input:
  `data-theme="<resolveTheme(theme)>"` (instead of `root.theme`) and, with a
  wallpaper, `style="--ml-bg-image:url(&quot;/bg/<tag>?v=<token>&quot;);--ml-bg-dim:0.7" data-bg="on"`.
  This applies to every `(app)` route (theme = `session.user.theme`, so a NULL column
  gives `discord` there even when `ml_skin` says otherwise) and to the public pages
  that render the OWNER's applier: `/[userCode]/[mediaCode]`, `/[userCode]` (tagless
  form renders the media page), `/u/[userCode]`, `/a/[userCode]/[slug]`,
  `/s/[code]/unlock`, `/s/[code]/[name]/unlock` (details in each route section).
- Response headers (`next.config.ts:141-226`, verified): every page gets
  `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin`,
  `Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains`,
  `X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN`,
  `Permissions-Policy: camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=(), usb=(), interest-cohort=()`
  and `Content-Security-Policy: <appCsp>` where (production)
  `appCsp = "default-src 'self'; base-uri 'self'; object-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'self'; form-action 'self'; img-src O data: blob:; media-src O blob:; frame-src O; font-src 'self' data:; worker-src 'self' blob:; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; connect-src O"`,
  `O = "'self'"` followed by `" " + origins` when non-empty, `origins` = the
  distinct `new URL(x).origin` of `APP_URL`, `SHORTLINK_URL`, each `APP_DOMAINS`
  entry, in that order, malformed entries skipped, joined by spaces
  (`next.config.ts:26-66`). Reference value: `O = 'self' http://localhost:8420`.
  The path `/authorize` (exactly, and `/authorize/...`) gets the SAME headers with
  `connect-src O http://127.0.0.1:*` instead (`next.config.ts:89-94,193-206`,
  verified). Dynamic pages are sent with
  `Cache-Control: private, no-cache, no-store, max-age=0, must-revalidate` and
  `Content-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8` (verified); `X-Powered-By` is not sent
  (PORT.md section 6).
  Not upstream: while the optional Cloudflare Turnstile check is on (section 11),
  `appCsp` and the `/authorize` policy carry ` https://challenges.cloudflare.com`
  at the end of `frame-src` and of `script-src`, on every page (the SPA reaches
  `/login` from any page without a new document); nothing else changes, and the
  raw/render policies never change.

### 5.13 Reference verification log

Heads (title, metas, links, order) and statuses checked against the reference:
`/login`, `/register`, `/forgot`, `/reset`, `/2fa`, `/authorize?port=1&state=x`
(+ `Accept-Language: pl`), `/invite/x`, `/welcome`, `/download` (no release
published), unmatched `/x/y/z/w`, not-found pages `/u/nonexistent`, `/x`, `/a/x/y`,
`/s/nonexistent/unlock`, `/s/nonexistent/foo/unlock`, and the public 404 media page
`/x/y` (also with a Discordbot user agent: identical). Redirects: `/`, `/dashboard`,
`/dashboard/admin` (with and without a fake cookie), `/authorize` (valid request, no
session, five encodings), trailing slashes, route precedence (`/download/x`,
`/dashboard/admin/x`, `/dashboard/admin/users/x`, `/s/x/unlock/y`), param spelling
(`/x%25`), malformed escapes (`/u/%E0%A4%A`). Root data: locale (7 cookie/header
combinations), mode and skin cookies (case, quotes, percent-encoding, duplicates,
undecodable values, `toString`), `timeZone` (`"UTC"` in the RSC payload). The head is
in `<head>` for curl, Chrome and Discordbot user agents alike. The browser-side head
of each not-found page was read from its inlined RSC payload. Signed-in pages and found public media could not be checked
(no sign-in allowed): their heads follow from the upstream code and Next's rules above.

---

## 6. Pages

| Route id | Section | Layouts | Gate | Metadata (en title) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `/` | 6.1 | root | redirect always | none |
| `/welcome` | 6.2 | root | none | `A` |
| `/download` | 6.3 | root | none | `Modlight App - A` |
| `/login` | 6.4 | root, (auth) | none | `Sign in - A` |
| `/register` | 6.5 | root, (auth) | none | `Create account - A` |
| `/forgot` | 6.6 | root, (auth) | none | `Forgot your password? - A` |
| `/reset` | 6.7 | root, (auth) | none | `Set a new password - A` |
| `/2fa` | 6.8 | root, (auth) | none | `Two-factor authentication - A` |
| `/invite/[token]` | 6.9 | root, (auth) | none | `Set up your account - A` |
| `/authorize` | 6.10 | root, (auth) | session after validation | `Connect a desktop app - A` + noindex |
| `/dashboard` | 6.11 | root, (app) | requireUser | `A` |
| `/dashboard/folders` | 6.12 | root, (app) | requireUser | `A` |
| `/dashboard/trash` | 6.13 | root, (app) | requireUser | `A` |
| `/dashboard/duplicates` | 6.14 | root, (app) | requireUser | `Duplicates - A` |
| `/dashboard/links` | 6.15 | root, (app) | requireUser | `Short links - A` |
| `/dashboard/paste` | 6.16 | root, (app) | layout only | `New paste - A` |
| `/dashboard/upload` | 6.17 | root, (app) | layout only | `Upload - A` |
| `/dashboard/profile` | 6.18 | root, (app) | requireUser | `Profile - A` |
| `/dashboard/tokens` | 6.19 | root, (app) | requireUser | `Integrations - A` |
| `/dashboard/admin` | 6.20 | root, (app) | requireAdmin | `Admin - A` |
| `/dashboard/admin/users/[id]/files` | 6.21 | root, (app) | requireAdmin | `User files - A` |
| `/u/[userCode]` | 6.22 | root | none | `<name> - A` |
| `/a/[userCode]/[slug]` | 6.23 | root | none | `<folder> - A` |
| `/s/[code]/unlock` | 6.24 | root | none | `Protected link - A` + noindex |
| `/s/[code]/[name]/unlock` | 6.25 | root | none | as 6.24 |
| `/[userCode]` | 6.26 | root | none | as 6.27 |
| `/[userCode]/[mediaCode]` | 6.27 | root | none (viewer relation) | `<file> · <brand>` (absolute) |

Every route section lists: (1) URL, layouts, gate; (2) inputs; (3) redirects and
notFound in evaluation order; (4) props; (5) metadata; (6) HTML document rules.
"Std" and the 404 rules are defined in sections 5.10 and 5.11.

### 6.1 `/`

1. URL `/`; layouts: root. No gate.
2. Inputs: none (the session only).
3. Always redirects (`app/page.tsx:11-14`): `getSession()` non-null ->
   `{redirect:"/dashboard",status:307}`, else `{redirect:"/login",status:307}`.
   A session lookup failure is an error payload (no catch here).
4. Props: none (`port:pages/page.tsx:10`, `RootPageProps = Record<string, never>`); the
   page is never rendered.
5. Metadata: none emitted (redirect).
6. Document: `307 Location: /login` (**verified**) or `/dashboard`.

### 6.2 `/welcome`

1. URL `/welcome`; layouts: root. No gate.
2. Inputs: none.
3. No redirect, no notFound.
4. Props (`port:pages/welcome/page.tsx:15-18`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `isAuthed` | `boolean` | `Boolean(await getSession())` (`app/welcome/page.tsx:12-13`; no catch: a lookup failure is an error payload) |

   `appName`/`appUrl` come from `layouts.root.config` (`app/welcome/page.tsx:14-15`).
5. Metadata: none -> `{title: A, tags: Std}` (root default title, not templated).
   **Verified**: `<title>Modlight</title>`, description D, application-name.
6. Document: 200, root `<html>` attributes.

### 6.3 `/download`

1. URL `/download` (static, wins over `/[userCode]`); layouts: root. No gate.
   `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"` (no effect here).
2. Inputs: none.
3. No redirect, no notFound.
4. Props (`port:pages/download/page.tsx:14-17`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `release` | `ReleaseManifest \| null` | `currentRelease()` (`lib/releases-core.ts:206-253`) |

   `ReleaseManifest` (`lib/releases-core.ts:43-54`, `port:overrides/lib/releases-core.ts:8-19`):
   `{name: string, version: string, url: string, filename: string, size: number,
   sha256: string | null, notes: string | null, publishedAt: string}`.

   `currentRelease()`:
   1. `raw = getSetting("release_app")` (`settings.value` of key `release_app`,
      `lib/settings.ts:76-79`); `parse(raw)` (`lib/releases-core.ts:180-195`): falsy ->
      null; `JSON.parse` failure -> null; `version` and `code` must be non-empty strings
      else null; `notes` kept only when a string; `publishedAt` kept when a string,
      else `""`. -> null means `release: null`.
   2. `SELECT code, ext, filename, size, checksum, published, owner_id, expires_at,
      password_hash FROM uploads WHERE code = $stored.code AND deleted_at IS NULL LIMIT 1`
      (`:210-224`; `code` is unique). No row, or `owner_id` NULL -> null.
   3. `!published || password_hash || expires_at` -> null (`:228`).
   4. `SELECT user_code, preferred_domain, skip_tag_in_url FROM "user" WHERE id = owner_id`
      (`:230-235`); missing -> null.
   5. `base = userBaseUrl(preferred_domain ?? null)` (`lib/media/urls.ts:22-27`: falsy
      or blank after trim/trailing-slash strip -> `config.appUrl`; else the
      `config.appDomains` entry equal to it case-insensitively, else `config.appUrl`);
      `tag = user_code ?? "u"`; `url = skip_tag_in_url ? base + "/" + code + "/raw" +
      (ext ? "." + ext : "") : base + "/" + tag + "/" + code + "/raw" + (ext ? "." + ext : "")`
      (`lib/media/urls.ts:58-65`, `lib/releases-core.ts:237-241`).
   6. `{name: "Modlight App", version: stored.version, url, filename: row.filename,
      size: Number(row.size), sha256: row.checksum ?? null, notes: stored.notes ?? null,
      publishedAt: stored.publishedAt}` (`:243-252`).

   Never sent: `password_hash` (used only as a gate) and the other selected columns.
5. Metadata (`app/download/page.tsx:12-20`; it calls `currentRelease()` itself):
   `{title: "Modlight App", description: release ? "Download Modlight App <version> for <A>." : "Modlight App for <A>."}`
   (`APP_NAME` is not translated). Result: title `Modlight App - <A>`; tags
   `[meta description=<that description>, meta application-name=A]`.
   **Verified** (no release published): `Modlight App - Modlight`,
   `Modlight App for Modlight.`
6. Document: 200, root attributes.

### 6.4 `/login`

1. URL `/login`; layouts: root, (auth) (`{}`). No gate (a signed-in visitor still sees
   the form).
2. Inputs: `next` (query). Upstream sanitised it on the server
   (`app/(auth)/login/page.tsx:14-18,25-26`): `raw = first(next)`; when `raw` starts with
   `/` and not with `//` -> `raw`, else `"/dashboard"`. The port does the same in the
   browser (`port:pages/(auth)/login/page.tsx:18-23,26-27`), so **the server reads no
   query parameter** here.
3. No redirect, no notFound.
4. Props (`port:pages/(auth)/login/page.tsx:13-16`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `registration` | `{open: boolean}` | `registrationPageState().open` (`app/(auth)/login/page.tsx:27-31`, `lib/registration.ts:173-190`) = `isFirstAccount() \|\| isRegistrationEnabled()` |

   - `isFirstAccount()` (`lib/registration.ts:161-164`): `SELECT count(*) FROM "user"` is 0.
   - `isRegistrationEnabled()` (`lib/settings.ts:134-142`): `settings` row
     `register_enabled`; value NULL or no row -> `config.auth.registrationEnabled`
     (env `REGISTRATION_ENABLED`, trimmed, `/^(1|true|yes|on)$/i`, default false,
     `lib/config.ts:8-11,257`); otherwise `value === "on" || "true" || "1"` (exact).
5. Metadata (`app/(auth)/login/page.tsx:8-11`): `{title: t("auth.login.title")}` ->
   `Sign in - A` (pl `Zaloguj się - A`); tags `Std`. **Verified** (en).
6. Document: 200.

### 6.5 `/register`

1. URL `/register`; layouts: root, (auth). No gate.
2. Inputs: none.
3. No redirect, no notFound (a closed registration renders the "closed" card).
4. Props (`port:pages/(auth)/register/page.tsx:12-15`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `state.bootstrap` | `boolean` | `isFirstAccount()` |
   | `state.open` | `boolean` | `bootstrap \|\| isRegistrationEnabled()` |
   | `state.keyRequired` | `boolean` | `isRegistrationKeyRequired() && !bootstrap` |

   (`lib/registration.ts:173-190`, all three as in 6.4.) `isRegistrationKeyRequired()`
   = `registrationKeyState() !== "unset"` (`:136-154`): `box = settings
   "registration_key_enc"`; empty/NULL -> `"unset"`; `tryDecryptSecret(box,
   "registration-key")` succeeds -> `"set"`, fails -> `"undecryptable"` (which counts
   as required) (`lib/secret-box.ts:117-127`). Never sent: the key or its ciphertext;
   only the three booleans leave the server.
5. Metadata (`app/(auth)/register/page.tsx:10-13`): `{title: t("auth.signUp")}` ->
   `Create account - A` (pl `Załóż konto - A`); tags `Std`. **Verified** (en).
6. Document: 200.

### 6.6 `/forgot`

1. URL `/forgot`; layouts: root, (auth). No gate.
2. Inputs: none.
3. No redirect, no notFound.
4. Props (`port:pages/(auth)/forgot/page.tsx:12-15`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `mailReady` | `boolean` | `(await loadMailSettings()).ready` (`app/(auth)/forgot/page.tsx:25`, `lib/mail-settings.ts:297-299`) |

   `loadMailSettings(force = false)` (`lib/mail-settings.ts:259-267`) answers from a
   per-process snapshot younger than 30 s (`TTL_MS`, `:246`), else reads the
   `settings` rows `smtp_host smtp_port smtp_security smtp_user smtp_password_enc
   mail_from mail_from_name email_verification_required` (`:33-42`, NULL values
   skipped, `lib/settings.ts:89-100`) and resolves (`resolveMailSettings`, `:103-180`): `smtp_host` trimmed
   non-empty -> `ready = passwordState !== "undecryptable" && (!user || passwordState === "set")`
   with `passwordState` = `"unset"` (no `smtp_password_enc`), `"set"` (decrypts with
   purpose `"smtp"`), `"undecryptable"`; else env `SMTP_HOST` trimmed non-empty ->
   `ready = true`; else `ready = false`. Only the boolean is sent.
5. Metadata (`app/(auth)/forgot/page.tsx:9-12`): `{title: t("auth.forgot.title")}` ->
   `Forgot your password? - A` (pl `Nie pamiętasz hasła? - A`); tags `Std`.
   **Verified** (en).
6. Document: 200.

### 6.7 `/reset`

1. URL `/reset`; layouts: root, (auth). No gate.
2. Inputs: `token`, `error` (Better Auth's reset link lands here with `?token=` or
   `?error=INVALID_TOKEN`). Upstream: `token = first(sp.token)`, `error =
   first(sp.error)`; `!token || error` renders the "expired" card
   (`app/(auth)/reset/page.tsx:13-28`). The port reads both in the browser
   (`port:pages/(auth)/reset/page.tsx:17-22`): **the server reads nothing**.
3. No redirect, no notFound.
4. Props: none (`ResetPageProps = Record<string, never>`).
5. Metadata (`app/(auth)/reset/page.tsx:8-11`): `{title: t("auth.reset.title")}` ->
   `Set a new password - A` (pl `Ustaw nowe hasło - A`); tags `Std`. **Verified** (en).
6. Document: 200.

### 6.8 `/2fa`

1. URL `/2fa`; layouts: root, (auth). No gate (the pending two-factor state lives in
   Better Auth's own cookie and is checked by the verify endpoints).
2. Inputs: `next`, sanitised exactly like `/login` (`app/(auth)/2fa/page.tsx:11-15,22`),
   in the browser by the port: **the server reads nothing**.
3. No redirect, no notFound.
4. Props: none (`TwoFactorPageProps = Record<string, never>`).
5. Metadata (`app/(auth)/2fa/page.tsx:6-9`): `{title: t("auth.twoFactor.title")}` ->
   `Two-factor authentication - A` (pl `Uwierzytelnianie dwuskładnikowe - A`); tags
   `Std`. **Verified** (en).
6. Document: 200.

### 6.9 `/invite/[token]`

1. URL `/invite/:token`; layouts: root, (auth). No gate.
2. Inputs: `params.token` in the page spelling (section 5.3:
   `encodeURIComponent(decoded segment)`). Real tokens are 32 random bytes in
   base64url (`lib/invites.ts:42`), which that spelling leaves unchanged.
3. No redirect, no notFound (an unknown or expired token renders the "expired" card;
   **verified** `/invite/x` -> 200).
4. Props (`port:pages/(auth)/invite/[token]/page.tsx:14-19`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `token` | `string` | `params.token`, page spelling (`app/(auth)/invite/[token]/page.tsx:27`) |
   | `preview` | `{email: string; name: string} \| null` | `previewInvite(token)` (`app/actions/invite.ts:25-28`) = `readInvite(token)` (`lib/invites.ts:95-122`) mapped to `{email, name}` |

   `readInvite(token)`: empty token -> null. `SELECT id, value, expires_at FROM
   verification WHERE identifier = 'invite:' || sha256_hex(utf8(token)) LIMIT 1`
   (`lib/invites.ts:25-30,98-102`); no row -> null. `expires_at <= now()` ->
   **`DELETE FROM verification WHERE id = row.id`**, null. `SELECT id, email, name FROM
   "user" WHERE id = row.value`; missing -> **same DELETE**, null. Else `{email, name}`.
   GET side effects: the two deletes. Upstream sent only `email` to the browser
   (`InviteForm token email`, `:57`); `name` reaches the browser only because the port
   type carries it (open question 12.10).
5. Metadata (`app/(auth)/invite/[token]/page.tsx:10-13`): `{title: t("auth.invite.title")}`
   in every branch -> `Set up your account - A` (pl `Dokończ zakładanie konta - A`);
   tags `Std`. **Verified** (en, expired branch).
6. Document: 200.

### 6.10 `/authorize`

1. URL `/authorize`; layouts: root, (auth). Gate: a session, but only AFTER the query
   validated (`app/(auth)/authorize/page.tsx:44-69`).
2. Inputs: `port`, `state`, `name` as raw query values (a string, or an array when the
   key repeats), parsed by `parseAuthorizeRequest` (`lib/authorize/loopback.ts:167-187`):
   - `single(v)`: only a non-repeated string counts; a repeated key is "absent"
     (`:83-86`).
   - port (`:96-102`): `single(port)?.trim()`; must match `/^[1-9][0-9]{3,4}$/` and lie
     in 1024..65535, else `{ok:false, reason:"invalid-port"}`.
   - state (`:114-118`): `single(state)?.trim()` matching `/^[A-Za-z0-9._~-]{8,128}$/`,
     else `{ok:false, reason:"invalid-state"}` (port is checked first).
   - clientName (`:134-144`): `single(name)`; empty -> `""`; else replace every
     `\p{C}` code point with a space, collapse `\s+` to one space, trim,
     `slice(0, 64)` in **UTF-16 code units**, trim again.
   - callback (`:159-164`): `"http://127.0.0.1:" + port + "/"`.
3. In order:
   1. invalid request -> render the "invalid" card; **no session lookup**.
   2. `getSession()` null -> `{redirect: "/login?next=" + encodeURIComponent(authorizePath(request)), status: 307}`
      where `authorizePath` (`:197-201`) = `"/authorize?" +` the
      `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` serialisation of `port`, `state` and, when
      non-empty, `name` = clientName (space -> `+`; only `A-Za-z0-9*-._` unescaped, so
      `~ ' ( )` become `%7E %27 %28 %29`); `encodeURIComponent` then leaves
      `A-Za-z0-9-_.!~*'()` and escapes the rest.
      **Verified**: `?port=8080&state=abcdefgh` ->
      `/login?next=%2Fauthorize%3Fport%3D8080%26state%3Dabcdefgh`;
      `name=My%20App%E2%80%AE!` -> `...%26name%3DMy%2BApp%2B%2521`;
      `state=abc~def.gh_-` -> `...state%3Dabc%257Edef.gh_-`;
      `port=%208080` -> valid (trimmed); `port=08080` and a repeated `port` -> 200
      invalid card.
4. Props (`port:pages/(auth)/authorize/page.tsx:18-23`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `parsed` | `{ok: true; request: {port: number; state: string; clientName: string; callback: string}} \| {ok: false; reason: "invalid-port" \| "invalid-state"}` | `parseAuthorizeRequest({port, state, name})` (`:47`) |
   | `session` | `{user: {email: string}} \| null` | `null` when `parsed.ok` is false (no lookup); otherwise `{user: {email: session.user.email}}` (`:78`) - never more of the session |
