   The page has no side effect (it mints nothing).
5. Metadata (`app/(auth)/authorize/page.tsx:26-34`), same in every branch:
   `{title: t("authorize.title"), robots: {index: false, follow: false}}` ->
   `Connect a desktop app - A` (pl `Podłącz aplikację na komputerze - A`); tags
   `[description D, application-name A, meta robots="noindex, nofollow"]`.
   **Verified** en and pl.
6. Document: 200 (or 307). Headers: the `/authorize` CSP variant (section 5.12),
   **verified** `connect-src 'self' http://localhost:8420 http://127.0.0.1:*`.

### Dashboard helpers (used by sections 6.11 to 6.21)

All `/dashboard...` routes: layouts root + `(app)` (section 5.8); gate order =
proxy gate (5.2 step 3), `(app)` layout `requireUser()`, then the page's own
`requireUser()`/`requireAdmin()`. All pages sit inside `app/(app)/dashboard/loading.tsx`
(status rules: section 5.11). Uncaught errors render `app/(app)/dashboard/error.tsx`
inside the panel (payload: `error` with `layouts` root and (app)).

**FileItem mapping "M"** (type `components/dashboard/types.ts:24-68`; mapping
`app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx:139-160`), from one `uploads` row `u`:

| Key | Type | Value |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | `u.id` |
| `code` | string | `u.code` |
| `filename` | string | `u.filename` |
| `ext` | string | `u.ext` |
| `mimeType` | string | `u.mime_type`, raw |
| `size` | number | `Number(u.size)` |
| `width`, `height`, `frames` | number \| null | as stored |
| `blurhash` | string \| null | as stored |
| `published` | boolean | as stored |
| `views`, `downloads` | number | as stored |
| `createdAt` | string | `u.created_at.toISOString()` |
| `kind` | FileKind | `kindOf(u.mime_type, u.kind, u.ext)` (WITH the extension, section 5.8 rule plus `MIME_BY_EXT[normalizeExt(ext)]` from `lib/media/sniff.ts:28-169` when the stored type is empty/useless) |
| `folderId` | string \| null | `u.folder_id ?? null` |
| `folderName` | string \| null | `u.folder_id ? (name of that id in listUserFolders(uid) ?? null) : null` (`:136-137,156`) |
| `expiresAt` | string \| null | `u.expires_at ? toISOString() : null` (always sent) |
| `maxViews` | number \| null | `u.max_views ?? null` (always sent) |
| `hasPassword` | boolean | `Boolean(u.password_hash)` (always sent) |

`durationSeconds` and `fps` (optional in the type) are NOT sent by these pages
(only `toFileItems`, `lib/data/uploads.ts:216-244`, used by the admin browser, adds
them). Never sent: `storage_key`, `thumb_key`, `delete_token`, `password_hash`,
`password_version`, `checksum`, `owner_id`, `deleted_at` and every other column not
listed.

**`listUploads(params)`** (`lib/data/uploads.ts:149-206`):
`page = pageOf(params.page)`: not a finite number -> 1, else
`min(1000000, max(1, floor(n)))`; `perPage = min(120, max(1, params.perPage ?? 30))`;
`sort = params.sort ?? "newest"`. WHERE (all ANDed): `owner_id = uid`;
`trashed ? deleted_at IS NOT NULL : deleted_at IS NULL`; if `search`:
`(filename ILIKE '%'||search||'%' OR code ILIKE '%'||search||'%')` (`%`, `_`, `\` in
the search are NOT escaped); folder: `folderId === null` -> `folder_id IS NULL`,
non-empty string + `includeSubfolders` -> `folder_id IN descendantFolderIds(uid, id)`
(DFS over the user's folders, always containing `id` itself, `lib/data/folders.ts:205-230`),
non-empty string -> `folder_id = id` (a non-uuid string is a Postgres error -> error
payload); kind (`:68-96`, on the RAW `mime_type`): `image/%`, `video/%`, `audio/%`
`LIKE`; document = `LIKE 'text/%' OR IN DOCUMENT_MIMES`; archive = `IN ARCHIVE_MIMES`;
paste = `kind = 'paste'`; other = none of those four `LIKE`s and `NOT IN`
(DOCUMENT_MIMES + ARCHIVE_MIMES); visibility public/private -> `published = true/false`;
`minSize` -> `size >= n`; `after` -> `created_at >= t`; `before` -> `created_at <= t`.
Then `SELECT count(*)::int` with that WHERE, and `SELECT * ... ORDER BY o LIMIT perPage
OFFSET (page-1)*perPage` with `o`: newest `created_at DESC` (default), oldest
`created_at ASC`, largest `size DESC`, smallest `size ASC`, name `filename ASC`,
views `views DESC` (single column, no tie-break). Returns
`{items, total: Number(count), page, perPage}` (the EFFECTIVE page and perPage).

**`userBaseUrl(preferred)`**: section 6.3 step 5. **`folderPath(uid, id)`**
(`lib/data/folders.ts:179-199`): `SELECT id, parent_id, name, slug FROM folders WHERE
owner_id = uid`, walk from `id` up the parents (visited-set guard), root first,
`[{id, name, slug}]`; `[]` when `id` is not one of them (exact string match).
**`unfiledStats(uid)`** (`:118-134`): `count(*)`, `coalesce(sum(size),0)` of `uploads`
with `owner_id = uid AND deleted_at IS NULL AND folder_id IS NULL`, both `Number`.

### 6.11 `/dashboard`

1. URL `/dashboard`; gate: section "Dashboard helpers", page `requireUser()`
   (`app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx:59`).
2. Inputs (all through `first()`; `app/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx:81-109`), all read by
   the server (the port receives everything as props):

   | Key | Rule |
   |---|---|
   | any key | `Object.keys(sp).length === 0` decides the bare-URL redirect (`:74`); any key, even empty, counts (the "All files" links use `?all=1`) |
   | `page` | `Math.max(1, Number(raw) \|\| 1)`, then `pageOf` in listUploads: finite >= 1 -> `floor`, capped at 1000000; NaN, Infinity, 0, negatives, 0.5 -> 1 |
   | `per` | `[30, 60, 120].includes(Number(raw)) ? Number(raw) : 30` (`" 60 "`, `"6e1"`, `"0x3c"` give 60) |
   | `search` | `raw?.trim() \|\| undefined` |
   | `sort` | one of `newest oldest largest smallest name views` (exact) else `"newest"` |
   | `view` | `raw === "list" ? "list" : "grid"` |
   | `folder` | `raw === "none" ? null : (raw \|\| undefined)`; any other string is used raw (no trim, no validation) |
   | `sub` | `raw === "1"` |
   | `kind` | one of the 7 FileKinds (exact) else undefined |
   | `visibility` | `"public"` or `"private"` else undefined |
   | `size` | `raw ? SIZE_PRESETS[raw] : undefined` with `10mb` = 10485760, `100mb` = 104857600, `1gb` = 1073741824 (`:31-35`) |
   | `after` | `parseDay(raw)` |
   | `before` | `parseDay(raw, true)` |

   `parseDay(value, endOfDay)` (`:45-51`): falsy -> undefined; when the raw value
   matches `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$`: `new Date(value + "T00:00:00")` = LOCAL midnight of
   the server time zone (upstream Docker image: UTC), and with `endOfDay`
   `setHours(23, 59, 59, 999)` local; any other value: JS `new Date(value)` (ISO forms
   with an offset or `Z` honour it, date-only ISO is UTC, date-time without offset is
   local, other strings use V8's legacy parser); an invalid date -> undefined.
3. In order: gates; `allFolders = listUserFolders(uid)` (always, `:64`); if the page
   query has no key and `allFolders.length > 0` -> `{redirect:"/dashboard/folders",status:307}`
   (`:74-76`). No notFound (an unknown or foreign folder id lists nothing).
4. Props (`port:pages/(app)/dashboard/page.tsx:22-61`), computed from
   `counts = kindCounts(uid)`, `result = listUploads({userId, page, perPage, search,
   sort, folderId, includeSubfolders, kind, visibility, minSize, after, before})`,
   `breadcrumb`, `prefs = getUserPrefs(uid)` (`:112-130`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `search?` | string | parsed `search`; omitted when undefined |
   | `sort` | SortKey | parsed |
   | `view` | `"grid" \| "list"` | parsed |
   | `kind?` | FileKind | parsed; omitted when invalid |
   | `visibility?` | `"public" \| "private"` | parsed |
   | `rawSize?` | string | `first(sp.size)` raw, omitted when the key is absent, `""` when empty |
   | `rawAfter?`, `rawBefore?` | string | `first(sp.after)`, `first(sp.before)` raw, same rule |
   | `folderId?` | string \| null | `null` for `none`, the raw string otherwise, **omitted** when undefined (null and absent render differently) |
   | `includeSubfolders` | boolean | parsed `sub` |
   | `filtering` | boolean | `Boolean(search \|\| kind \|\| visibility \|\| minSize \|\| after \|\| before)` with PARSED values (`:162`) |
   | `counts` | `Record<FileKind, number>` | `kindCounts(uid)` (section 5.8) |
   | `result` | `{total, page, perPage}` (numbers) | from `listUploads` (effective values) |
   | `items` | `FileItem[]` | mapping M over `result.items` |
   | `breadcrumb` | `{id, name, slug}[]` | `folderId ? folderPath(uid, folderId) : []` (`:128`) |
   | `currentFolder?` | `{id, name, slug, isPublic}` | `folderId ? allFolders.find(f => f.id === folderId) : undefined` (`:136`), exact match, only these 4 keys; omitted when none |
   | `linkBase` | string | `userBaseUrl(prefs?.preferredDomain ?? null)` (`:134`) |
   | `userCode` | string | `session.user.userCode ?? ""` (`:164`) |
   | `copyRaw` | boolean | `session.user.copyRaw ?? false` (`:165`) |

   Never sent: `prefs` fields other than through `linkBase`.
5. Metadata: none -> `{title: A, tags: Std}`.
6. Document: 200 (redirect: section 5.11 boundary rule). No side effects.

### 6.12 `/dashboard/folders`

1. URL `/dashboard/folders`; page `requireUser()` (`app/(app)/dashboard/folders/page.tsx:57`).
2. Inputs: `folder` only: `folderId = first(sp.folder) || null` (`:64`; no trim, `none`
   has no special meaning). Read by the server.
3. In order: gates; `rows = listUserFolders(uid)`, `breadcrumb = folderId ?
   folderPath(uid, folderId) : []`, `unsorted = folderId ? null : unfiledStats(uid)`
   (`:66-70`); `tree = buildFolderTree(rows)`; `current = folderId ? findNode(tree,
   folderId) : undefined` (depth-first, exact id, `:32-39,73`); **`folderId && !current`
   -> notFound** (`:74`): unknown or foreign ids, other uuid spellings, non-uuid
   strings, folders unreachable from a root. No redirect.
4. Props (`port:pages/(app)/dashboard/folders/page.tsx:15-28`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `current` | `{id, name, slug, isPublic, count, size} \| null` | the found node (direct `count`/`size`, not totals); `null` at the root |
   | `breadcrumb` | `{id, name, slug}[]` | above |
   | `children` | `BrowserFolder[]` | `(current ? current.children : tree).map(toBrowserFolder)` (`:18-30,76`): `{id, name, slug, isPublic, directCount: node.count, fileCount: node.totalCount, size: node.totalSize, subfolderCount: node.children.length, descendantCount}` with `descendantCount` = folders at any depth below (`:13-15`); type `components/dashboard/FolderCard.tsx:11-26` |
   | `totalFolders` | number | `rows.length` (all folders, unreachable ones included, `:79`) |
   | `unsorted` | `{count, size} \| null` | above |
   | `userCode` | string | `session.user.userCode ?? ""` (`:77`) |

5. Metadata: none -> `{title: A, tags: Std}` (notFound: section 5.11, i.e. the same
   plus `robots: "noindex"`).
6. Document: 200 (notFound inside the boundary: section 5.11). No side effects.

### 6.13 `/dashboard/trash`

1. URL `/dashboard/trash`; page `requireUser()` (`app/(app)/dashboard/trash/page.tsx:15`).
2. Inputs: none.
3. No redirect, no notFound. `trashTotalsFor` throws (error payload) when no `user`
   row has `user_code = tag` or that row is not the viewer (`lib/uploads.ts:472-485`).
4. Props (`port:pages/(app)/dashboard/trash/page.tsx:16-25`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `tag` | string | `session.user.userCode ?? ""` (`:22`) |
   | `linkBase` | string | `userBaseUrl(prefs?.preferredDomain ?? null)` (`:39`) |
   | `totals` | `{files: number; bytes: number} \| null` | `tag ? trashTotalsFor({userId, email}, tag) : null` (`:34`; `lib/uploads.ts:510-527`): owner = `SELECT id FROM "user" WHERE user_code = tag LIMIT 1` (must be the viewer), then `count(*)`, `coalesce(sum(size),0)` of `uploads WHERE owner_id = id AND deleted_at IS NOT NULL`, both `Number` (the whole trash) |
   | `items` | `TrashItem[]` | `listUploads({userId, trashed: true, perPage: 120, sort: "newest"})` (`:25-30`): newest by `created_at` (upload time, not deletion time), first 120; mapping M with `folderId = u.folder_id`, `folderName = null`, plus `daysLeft = trashDaysLeft(u.deleted_at, now)` (`:43-69`) |

   `TrashItem` = FileItem + `daysLeft: number | null`
   (`components/dashboard/TrashList.tsx:36-38`). `trashDaysLeft`
   (`lib/maintenance/trash.ts:52-57`): `null` when `deleted_at` is null/invalid, else
   `max(0, ceil((deleted_at_ms + 30*86400000 - now) / 86400000))`, `now` = one
   `Date.now()` for the whole render. `user.email` is used only as the actor, never
   sent.
5. Metadata: none -> `{title: A, tags: Std}`.
6. Document: 200. No side effects.

### 6.14 `/dashboard/duplicates`

1. URL `/dashboard/duplicates`; page `requireUser()` (`app/(app)/dashboard/duplicates/page.tsx:25`).
2. Inputs: none.
3. No redirect, no notFound.
4. Props (`port:pages/(app)/dashboard/duplicates/page.tsx:13-18`):
   `userCode: string` = `session.user.userCode ?? ""` (`:51`);
   `scan: DuplicateScan` = `scanDuplicates(uid)` (`:27`; `lib/data/duplicates.ts:607-685`).
   Types (`lib/data/duplicates.ts:53-142`, mirrored in `port:overrides/lib/data/duplicates.ts:50-139`):
   - `DuplicateScan` = `{groups: DuplicateGroup[], totalGroups, totalFiles,
     reclaimable, dismissedGroups: number, active: {groups, redundant, reclaimable},
     truncated: boolean, unchecksummed: number, scannedAt: string}`;
   - `DuplicateGroup` = `{checksum: string, copies, size, bytes, reclaimable: number,
     dismissed: boolean, files: DuplicateFile[]}`;
   - `DuplicateFile` = `{id, code, filename, ext, mimeType: string, size: number,
     createdAt: string, folderId: string | null, folderName: string | null, views,
     downloads: number, url: string, blurhash: string | null}` (all keys present).

   `scanDuplicates(uid)`:
   1. concurrently: the aggregate (`:379-425`) = the `dupes` CTE of section 5.8 plus
      `marked` (`LEFT JOIN duplicate_dismissals` -> `dismissed`), selecting
      `checksum, copies, bytes, keep, dismissed` and the window totals
      `count(*) over()` total_groups, `sum(copies) over()` total_files,
      `sum(bytes-keep) over()` total_reclaimable, and over the not-dismissed rows
      `count(*)` active_groups, `coalesce(sum(copies-1),0)` active_redundant,
      `coalesce(sum(bytes-keep),0)` active_reclaimable,
      `ORDER BY dismissed ASC, (bytes-keep) DESC, checksum LIMIT 100`;
      `getUserPrefs(uid)`; `countUnchecksummed` (`lib/upload/duplicate-lookup.ts:156-170`:
      live uploads with `checksum IS NULL OR checksum = ''`);
   2. no aggregate rows -> `emptyScan(unchecksummed)` (`:311-323`): zeros, `[]`,
      `active = {0,0,0}`, `truncated = false`, `scannedAt = now ISO`;
   3. totals from the first aggregate row through `num()` (`Number(v ?? 0)`,
      non-finite -> 0) (`:617-632`);
   4. files: `SELECT u.id, code, filename, ext, mime_type, size, created_at,
      folder_id, f.name AS folder_name, views, downloads, checksum, blurhash FROM
      uploads u LEFT JOIN folders f ON f.id = u.folder_id WHERE u.owner_id = uid AND
      deleted_at IS NULL AND checksum IN (<the <=100 checksums>) ORDER BY checksum,
      created_at, id LIMIT 2000` (`:634-660`);
   5. `bucketByChecksum` (`:195-265`): drop rows with a blank checksum; group by
      checksum; sort each group by `createdAt` (ms) then `id.localeCompare`;
      `copies = stat.copies ?? n`; skip groups with `copies < 2` or fewer than 2 rows;
      `bytes = stat.bytes`, `size` = the OLDEST file's size,
      `reclaimable = max(0, bytes - size)`; `files` = first 50 rows with
      `createdAt` ISO and `url = mediaUrls(prefs, code, ext).url`
      (`lib/media/urls.ts:93-105`: `base = userBaseUrl(prefs.preferredDomain)`,
      `skipTagInUrl === true` -> `base/code[.ext]`, else `base/(userCode ?? "u")/code[.ext]`);
      groups sorted not-dismissed first, then `reclaimable` DESC, then
      `checksum.localeCompare`;
   6. `dismissedGroups = max(0, totalGroups - active.groups)`,
      `truncated = totalGroups > groups.length`, `scannedAt = new Date().toISOString()`
      (`:674-684`).

   `checksum` (sha256 hex) is sent, as upstream does; storage keys never.
5. Metadata (`:10-13`): `{title: t("profile.duplicates.page.metaTitle")}` ->
   `Duplicates - A` (pl `Duplikaty - A`); tags `Std`.
6. Document: 200. No side effects (read-only).

### 6.18 `/dashboard/profile`

1. URL `/dashboard/profile`; page `requireUser()` (`app/(app)/dashboard/profile/page.tsx:68`;
   `myUrlPrefs()` and `listMySessions()` resolve the same session again).
2. Inputs: none (no params, no query).
3. No redirect besides the gates, no notFound.
4. Props (`port:pages/(app)/dashboard/profile/page.tsx:33-63`), from
   `stats = userStats(uid)`, `prefs = getUserPrefs(uid)`, `tree = folderTree(uid)`,
   `urlPrefs = myUrlPrefs()`, `sessions = listMySessions()` (`:74-82`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `u` | `{name: string; email: string; userCode: string \| null; hideUploads: boolean \| null; copyRaw: boolean \| null; usedQuota: number \| null; maxQuota: number \| null; role: string \| null; theme: string \| null; locale: string \| null; twoFactorEnabled: boolean \| null; emailVerified: boolean}` | `session.user` columns `name, email, user_code, hide_uploads, copy_raw, used_quota, max_quota, role, theme, locale, two_factor_enabled, email_verified` (raw; the page applies its own defaults) |
   | `stats` | `{count, totalSize, totalViews}` (numbers) | `userStats(uid)` (section 5.8; `totalViews = coalesce(sum(views),0)::int`) |
   | `prefs` | `{userCode: string \| null; skipTagInUrl: boolean; defaultFolderId: string \| null; backgroundDim: number} \| null` | `getUserPrefs(uid)` (`lib/data/user-prefs.ts:39-63`): `Boolean(skip_tag_in_url)`, `background_dim ?? 70`; null when the row is missing; only these four keys |
   | `urlPrefs` | `{scheme, pattern: string \| null, wordCount: number, randomLength: number, preferredDomain: string \| null, baseUrl: string}` | `myUrlPrefs()` (`app/actions/account.ts:294-319`): `SELECT naming_scheme, naming_pattern, naming_word_count, naming_random_length, preferred_domain FROM "user" WHERE id = uid`; `scheme` = the stored value when it is exactly one of `random words random-chars filename filename-random date-filename custom` (`lib/naming.ts:175-189`) else `"random"`; `pattern ?? null`; `wordCount` NULL -> 2 else `Math.round` clamped to 2..4; `randomLength` NULL -> 6 else rounded, clamped to 5..32 (`lib/naming.ts:210-227`); `preferredDomain` raw `?? null`; `baseUrl = userBaseUrl(preferredDomain)` |
   | `sessions` | `SessionView[]` | `listMySessions()` (`lib/data/sessions.ts:186-215`), below |
   | `folders` | `{id: string; name: string; depth: number}[]` | `flattenFolders(tree)` (`:31-36`): pre-order, roots depth 0, child = parent + 1 |
   | `avatarUrl` | `string \| null` | `avatarUrl(prefs?.userCode ?? u.userCode, u.image)` (section 5.8) |
   | `backgroundUrl` | `string \| null` | `backgroundUrl(prefs?.userCode, prefs?.backgroundKey)` (`lib/background.ts:99-106`; no fallback to the session tag) |

   `SessionView` (`lib/data/sessions.ts:22-63`) = `{id: string, current: boolean,
   createdAt: string, renewedAt: string, expiresAt: string, ip: string | null,
   userAgent: string | null, device: {browser: string | null, os: string | null,
   kind: "desktop" | "mobile" | "tablet" | "client" | "unknown"}, impersonated:
   boolean}`. Rows: Better Auth `listSessions` = `SELECT * FROM session WHERE user_id =
   uid AND expires_at > now()`; per row `id`, `current = id === <request session id>`,
   ISO of `created_at`, `updated_at`, `expires_at`; `ip = ip_address` trimmed, first
   64 characters, null when blank; `userAgent` = first 300 UTF-16 units, null when
   empty; `device = describeDevice(userAgent)` (`:66-171`: clients
   `Modlight[/ ]?([\d.]+)?` "Modlight App", `ShareX[/ ]?([\d.]+)?`, `curl\/`, `Wget\/`,
   `python-requests\/`, `PostmanRuntime\/` "Postman", `insomnia\/` "Insomnia",
   `okhttp\/` "OkHttp", `Go-http-client\/` "Go", `PowerShell\/` -> kind `client`,
   browser `name + " " + version`; else browsers `Edg(?:iOS|A)?\/(\d+)` Edge,
   `OPR\/` and `Opera\/` Opera, `Vivaldi\/`, `SamsungBrowser\/` "Samsung Internet",
   `(?:Firefox|FxiOS)\/` Firefox, `(?:CriOS|Chrome)\/` Chrome,
   `Version\/(\d+)[\d.]*(?: Mobile\/\S+)? Safari` Safari, first match wins, e.g.
   `Chrome 141`; os: `Windows NT 6.1/6.2/6.3` -> Windows 7/8/8.1 else Windows,
   `CrOS` ChromeOS, `Android N`, `iOS N`, `Mac OS X|Macintosh` macOS, `Linux|X11`
   Linux; kind: `iPad|Tablet|Android(?!.*Mobile)` tablet, `Mobile|iPhone|iPod|Android`
   mobile, an os -> desktop, else unknown); `impersonated = Boolean(impersonated_by)`.
   Order: the current session first, then `createdAt` descending (`:211-214`).

   Never sent: `user.id`, `user.image` (storage key), `background_key`,
   `default_password_hash`, any session `token`.
5. Metadata (`:28`): `{title: "Profile"}` (not translated) -> `Profile - A`; tags `Std`.
6. Document: 200. No side effects.
