### 6.20 `/dashboard/admin`

1. URL `/dashboard/admin`; page `requireAdmin()` (`app/(app)/dashboard/admin/page.tsx:79`):
   no session -> `/login`, `role !== "admin"` (NULL included) -> `/dashboard` (inside
   the boundary: section 5.11; seen on the reference as 200 + client redirect,
   section 2.1).
2. Inputs: none (the tab is client state).
3. No notFound.
4. Props (`port:pages/(app)/dashboard/admin/page.tsx:35-52`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `session` | `{user: {id: string; email: string}}` | `session.user.id`, `session.user.email` only |
   | `regEnabled` | boolean | `isRegistrationEnabled()` (section 6.4) |
   | `keyState` | `"unset" \| "set" \| "undecryptable"` | `registrationKeyState()` (section 6.5; box `v1.<iv>.<tag>.<ct>` base64url, AES-256-GCM, key = HKDF-SHA256(ikm = base64 `SETTINGS_ENCRYPTION_KEY` when set (must be 32 bytes) else the UTF-8 auth secret, salt `modlight/secret-box/2026`, info `modlight:registration-key:v1`), `lib/secret-box.ts:36-127`) |
   | `mail` | `MailStatus` | `toMailStatus(await loadMailSettings(true))` (`:86`; `lib/mail-settings.ts:183-197`), below |
   | `stats` | `GlobalStats` | `loadGlobalStats()` (`:45-76`), below |
   | `disk` | `ServerStorageUsage` | `serverStorageUsage()` (`lib/data/admin.ts:161-186`), below |
   | `diskInsights` | `DiskInsights` | `diskInsights()` (`lib/data/admin-disk.ts:228-399`), which upstream's `<ServerDisk>` awaited itself (`components/admin/ServerDisk.tsx:46-51`; port `port:overrides/components/admin/ServerDisk.tsx`) |
   | `defaultQuota` | `string \| null` | `DEFAULT_USER_QUOTA` via `int(v, -1)` (`lib/config.ts:13-16,287`): negative -> null, else `humanFileSize(n)` (`:90-92`) |
   | `turnstile` | `TurnstileStatus` | Not upstream: `{source: "panel" \| "env" \| "none", enabled, active: boolean, siteKey: string, secretState: "unset" \| "set" \| "undecryptable", envAvailable: boolean}` of the optional Cloudflare Turnstile check, read through like `mail` (`server/src/turnstile.rs`); the card under Settings (section 11) |

   - `MailStatus` = `{source: "panel" | "env" | "none", host, port: number, security:
     "none" | "starttls" | "tls", user, from, fromName: string, ready: boolean,
     passwordState: "unset" | "set" | "undecryptable", verificationRequired: boolean,
     envAvailable: boolean}`; `force = true` bypasses the 30 s snapshot and REPLACES
     it (the snapshot also feeds Better Auth's `requireEmailVerification`). Panel
     branch (trimmed `smtp_host` non-empty): `host`; `port = Number(smtp_port)` when
     finite, > 0 and < 65536 (truncated) else 587; `security` = `smtp_security` when
     `tls`/`starttls`/`none` else `starttls`; `user` trimmed; `passwordState` from
     `smtp_password_enc` (purpose `smtp`); `ready = passwordState !== "undecryptable"
     && (!user || passwordState === "set")`; `from = mail_from` trimmed `||`
     `MAIL_FROM` (default `no-reply@localhost`); `fromName = mail_from_name` trimmed
     `|| A`; `verificationRequired = isOn(email_verification_required) && ready`. Env
     branch (`SMTP_HOST` trimmed non-empty): `port = int(SMTP_PORT, 587)`, `security` =
     `SMTP_SECURITY` (trimmed, lower-cased) when valid else `tls` if `SMTP_SECURE`
     matches `/^(1|true|yes|on)$/i` else `none`, `user = SMTP_USER` untrimmed,
     `fromName = MAIL_FROM_NAME || A`, `ready = true`, `passwordState` set/unset by
     `SMTP_PASSWORD`. None branch: `{source: "none", host: "", port: 587, security:
     "starttls", user: "", from: MAIL_FROM, fromName: A, ready: false, passwordState:
     "unset", verificationRequired: false}`. Always `envAvailable =
     Boolean(SMTP_HOST.trim())`. Never sent: any password, encrypted or not.
   - `GlobalStats` (`components/admin/StatsCards.tsx:26-36`) = `{totalFiles, totalViews,
     users, admins, banned, orphanFiles: number, totalStorage, orphanStorage: string}`
     from `SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE owner_id IS NOT NULL)::int, coalesce(sum(size)
     FILTER (WHERE owner_id IS NOT NULL),0), coalesce(sum(views),0)::int,
     count(*) FILTER (WHERE owner_id IS NULL)::int, coalesce(sum(size) FILTER (WHERE
     owner_id IS NULL),0) FROM uploads WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` and `SELECT
     count(*)::int, count(*) FILTER (WHERE role = 'admin')::int, count(*) FILTER
     (WHERE banned)::int FROM "user"`; numbers through `Number(x ?? 0)`, the two
     storage values through `humanFileSize`.
   - `ServerStorageUsage` = `{driver: string, liveFiles, liveBytes, trashedFiles,
     trashedBytes: number, disk: {totalBytes, freeBytes, usedBytes} | null,
     minFreeBytes: number}`: one filtered aggregate over ALL uploads (live = `deleted_at
     IS NULL`); `driver = (STORAGE_DRIVER || "local").toLowerCase()`; `disk` = statfs of
     `STORAGE_LOCAL_PATH || "./data/storage"` for the local driver only (`total =
     blocks*bsize`, `free = bavail*bsize`, `used = (blocks-bfree)*bsize`, null on
     error; `lib/upload/disk.ts:43-65`); `minFreeBytes = int(MIN_FREE_DISK_BYTES,
     10737418240)`. The path is never sent.
   - `DiskInsights` (`port:overrides/lib/data/admin-disk.ts:25-114`) = `{driver,
     local: boolean, kinds: {kind: MediaKind, files, bytes}[], kindBytes, accounts:
     AccountUsage[], biggest: HeavyFile[], cold: HeavyFile[], coldTotals: {files,
     bytes, days}, trash: {files, bytes, oldest: string | null, dueSoonFiles,
     dueSoonBytes, overdueFiles, overdueBytes}}`, all statements in one REPEATABLE
     READ, READ ONLY transaction, `now` = render time: cutoffs cold = now - 180 d,
     purge = now - 30 d, dueSoon = now - 30 d + 7 d; scalars over all uploads (trash =
     `deleted_at IS NOT NULL`, `oldest = min(deleted_at)`, dueSoon = `deleted_at <
     dueSoon AND >= purge`, overdue = `< purge`, cold = live and
     `coalesce(last_accessed_at, created_at) < cold`); `kinds` = live uploads grouped by
     `(mime_type, ext)`, mapped with `mediaKind`, summed per kind, sorted bytes desc
     then kind asc, `kindBytes` = their sum; `accounts` = `GROUP BY owner_id` with live
     and trashed counts/bytes (NULL owner = orphaned), stable-sorted by total bytes
     desc, first 6 kept and the rest folded into one `{rest: true, accounts: n}` row
     (an orphan group ranked 7th or lower is folded too), names from `SELECT id, name,
     email, user_code FROM "user" WHERE id IN (kept ids)`; `AccountUsage = {id, name,
     email, tag: string | null, orphaned, rest: boolean, accounts, liveFiles, liveBytes,
     trashedFiles, trashedBytes: number}`; `biggest` = 6 live uploads by `size DESC`,
     `cold` = 6 cold live uploads by `coalesce(last_accessed_at, created_at) ASC`, both
     `LEFT JOIN "user"`, `HeavyFile = {id, code, filename, ext (?? ""), mimeType, size,
     ownerId, ownerTag, ownerName, createdAt (ISO | null), views, lastAccessedAt (ISO |
     null)}`; `coldTotals.days = 180`. Owner ids and e-mails are included by design
     (admin only).
5. Metadata (`:33`): `{title: "Admin"}` (not translated) -> `Admin - A`; tags `Std`.
6. Document: 200. No database writes (the disk insights run read-only); side effect:
   the process-wide mail snapshot is refreshed.

### 6.21 `/dashboard/admin/users/[id]/files`

1. URL `/dashboard/admin/users/:id/files`; page `requireAdmin()` FIRST
   (`app/(app)/dashboard/admin/users/[id]/files/page.tsx:55`).
2. Inputs: `params.id` (page spelling; compared as text with `user.id`, so a strange
   value simply matches nothing; Better Auth ids are 32 `[A-Za-z0-9]`). Query, all via
   `first()` (`:60-72`): `page` and `per` exactly as `/dashboard` (6.11); `search`
   `?.trim() || undefined`; `sort` one of the six SortKeys else `newest`; `kind` one of
   the seven FileKinds else undefined; `view = raw === "list" ? "list" : "grid"`;
   `trashed = raw === "1"`. All read by the server.
3. In order: gates; `requireAdmin`; `adminUserFiles(adminId, id, {...})`
   (`lib/data/admin-files.ts:166-222`) returns null when `SELECT id, name, email,
   user_code, image, role, banned, preferred_domain, copy_raw, used_quota, max_quota
   FROM "user" WHERE id = $id LIMIT 1` finds nothing -> **notFound** (`:82`; inside the
   boundary, 200 + robots noindex, section 2.1). No audit row on that path.
4. Props (`port:pages/(app)/dashboard/admin/users/[id]/files/page.tsx:25-38`):
   `search?` (omitted when undefined), `sort`, `kind?`, `view`, `trashed` (the parsed
   values) and `data: AdminFilesView` (`port:overrides/lib/data/admin-files.ts:28-52`) =
   `{target, items: FileItem[], trashItems: TrashItem[] | null, total, page, perPage,
   liveTotal: number}`:
   - `target` = `{id, name, email, tag: user_code ?? "", avatar: avatarUrl(user_code,
     image), role: role ?? null, banned: banned ?? false, linkBase:
     userBaseUrl(preferred_domain) (the TARGET's domain), copyRaw: copy_raw ?? false,
     usedQuota: Number(used_quota ?? 0), maxQuota: Number(max_quota ?? -1)}`
     (`lib/data/admin-files.ts:90-125`);
   - `items` = `toFileItems(rows, folderNames)` (mapping M plus `durationSeconds`, `fps`;
     `folderName` from `SELECT id, name FROM folders WHERE owner_id = $id`) over
     `listUploads({userId: id, page, perPage, search, sort, kind, trashed})` (no folder
     or visibility filter);
   - `trashItems` = null unless `trashed`; then `items[i]` + `daysLeft =
     trashDaysLeft(deleted_at, now)` with `now` taken after the queries;
   - `total`, `page`, `perPage` from `listUploads` (effective values); `liveTotal =
     SELECT count(*)::int FROM uploads WHERE owner_id = $id AND deleted_at IS NULL`.

   Never sent: the target's `image` key, `preferred_domain`, password hashes and the
   other secret upload columns.
5. Metadata (`:20-23`): `{title: t("admin.files.pageTitle")}` -> `User files - A`
   (pl `Pliki użytkownika - A`); tags `Std` (+ robots `noindex` on notFound).
6. **Side effect**: every render whose target exists writes an audit entry unless one
   exists (`lib/data/admin-files.ts:135-157,174`): `SELECT id FROM audit_log WHERE
   user_id = $adminId AND action = 'admin.user.files_viewed' AND created_at > now - 10
   min AND detail ->> 'targetUserId' = $target.id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`;
   no hit -> `INSERT INTO audit_log (user_id, action, detail, ip) VALUES ($adminId,
   'admin.user.files_viewed', {"by": adminId, "targetUserId": target.id,
   "targetEmail": target.email}, <x-real-ip trimmed or "unknown">)` (`lib/audit.ts:18-32`;
   insert errors are logged and swallowed, e.g. an ip over varchar(64); no lock between
   the check and the insert). Document: 200.

### Public page helpers (used by sections 6.22 to 6.27)

- Layouts: root only; no gate; the viewer's session matters only on the media page.
- `PublicTileItem` (`components/public/types.ts:8-19`):
  `{id: string, code: string, ext: string, filename: string, mimeType: string, size: number, blurhash: string | null}`.
- **Owner theme in the document** (section 5.12): these pages render the owner's
  `<ThemeApplier>`, so the document carries `data-theme = resolveTheme(<owner theme>)`
  and the owner's wallpaper (section 2.2 lists the prop per page).
- Image URLs for cards: `rawUrl(tag, code, ext, base)` = `base/tag/code/raw.ext`
  (`base/tag/code/raw` when `ext` is empty) (`lib/media/urls.ts:63-65`).
- `isDisplayableImage(mime, ext)` (`lib/media/mime.ts:9-19,55-60`): `effectiveType`
  (section "Dashboard helpers", mapping M) is one of `image/apng image/bmp image/gif
  image/x-icon image/jpeg image/png image/tiff image/webp image/avif`.
- ICU messages with `{count, plural, ...}` follow CLDR plural rules of the locale
  (pl: one / few / many / other) and print `#` with `Intl.NumberFormat(locale)`.

### 6.22 `/u/[userCode]` (public profile)

1. URL `/u/:userCode`; layouts: root. No gate: no session is read; owner and admin
   get the same answer.
2. Inputs: `params.userCode` (exact, case-sensitive match; the page sees the encoded
   spelling, `generateMetadata` the decoded one; tags are `[a-z0-9...]`, so both
   match the same rows in practice). No query, cookie or header (except the locale).
3. `loadGallery(userCode)` (`app/u/[userCode]/page.tsx:44-107`), run by both the page
   and `generateMetadata`:
   1. `SELECT id, name, user_code, theme, preferred_domain, public_profile,
      background_key, background_dim, image FROM "user" WHERE user_code = $userCode
      LIMIT 1` (no alias fallback); no row or `user_code` NULL -> null;
   2. `public_profile` not true -> null (`:75`);
   3. items: `SELECT id, code, ext, filename, mime_type, size, blurhash FROM uploads
      WHERE owner_id = $id AND published = true AND deleted_at IS NULL AND kind = 'file'
      AND password_hash IS NULL AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now()) AND
      (max_views IS NULL OR views < max_views) ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 200`
      (`:77-104`, `ITEM_LIMIT` `:23`).

   null -> **notFound** (`:162`). No redirect.
4. Props (`port:pages/u/[userCode]/page.tsx:19-30`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `owner` | `{name: string; userCode: string; theme: string \| null}` | `user.name`, `user.user_code`, `user.theme` raw |
   | `items` | `PublicTileItem[]` | the items query, columns as selected (`size` a number) |
   | `ownerBase` | string | `userBaseUrl(preferred_domain)` (`:167`) |
   | `ownerAvatar` | `string \| null` | `avatarUrl(user_code, image)` (`:169`, section 5.8) |
   | `background` | `{url, dim} \| null` | `backgroundStyle(user_code, background_key, background_dim)` (`:175`, section 5.8) |

   Never sent: the owner's `id`, `image` key, `background_key`, raw `preferred_domain`,
   `public_profile`, `email`.
5. Metadata (`:113-153`):
   - not found: `{title: t("public.profile.notFoundTitle"), robots: {index: false, follow: false}}`
     (en `Not found`, pl `Nie znaleziono`) - only the browser-side head; the payload
     uses the notFound `meta` of section 5.11.
   - found: with `title = t("public.profile.metaTitle", {name})` (= the name),
     `description = t("public.profile.metaDescription", {tag: user_code})`
     (en `Public gallery · @<tag>`, pl `Publiczna galeria · @<tag>`),
     `url = ownerBase + "/u/" + user_code`,
     `ogImage = rawUrl(user_code, first.code, first.ext, ownerBase) + "?w=1200&h=630"`
     for the FIRST item (in the list order) that `isDisplayableImage`, else none:
     ```ts
     { title, description, alternates: { canonical: url },
       openGraph: { type: "profile", title, description, siteName: config.embedBrand, url,
         images: ogImage ? [{ url: ogImage, width: 1200, height: 630, alt: title }] : undefined },
       twitter: { card: ogImage ? "summary_large_image" : "summary", title, description,
         images: ogImage ? [ogImage] : undefined } }
     ```
     `meta = {title: "<name> - A", description, applicationName: A, robots: null}`.
     Tags, in order: `<title><name> - A</title>`; `meta description=<description>`;
     `meta application-name=A`; `link rel=canonical href=<url>`;
     `meta property=og:title content=<name>`; `og:description`; `og:url=<url>`;
     `og:site_name=<embedBrand>`; with an image: `og:image=<ogImage, WHATWG-normalised>`,
     `og:image:width=1200`, `og:image:height=630`, `og:image:alt=<name>`;
     `og:type=profile`; `meta name=twitter:card content=summary_large_image|summary`;
     `twitter:title=<name>`; `twitter:description`; with an image
     `twitter:image=<ogImage, normalised>`.
6. Document: 200, `data-theme = resolveTheme(owner.theme)` (NULL -> `discord`) and
   the owner's wallpaper. No side effects (SELECTs only). Not-found: section 5.11
   (**verified** `/u/nonexistent`: 404, error-shell head = `<title>Modlight</title>`
   + robots `noindex` + Std; RSC head `Not found - Modlight` + robots
   `noindex, nofollow`).

### 6.23 `/a/[userCode]/[slug]` (public folder)

1. URL `/a/:userCode/:slug`; layouts: root. No gate, no viewer bypass.
2. Inputs: `params.userCode`, `params.slug` (exact; slugs are `[a-z0-9-]`,
   `lib/naming.ts:48-60`). Locale only.
3. `getPublicFolder(userCode, slug)` (`lib/data/folders.ts:253-334`), run by both the
   page and `generateMetadata`:
   1. owner: `SELECT id, user_code, name, theme, preferred_domain, background_key,
      background_dim FROM "user" WHERE user_code = $userCode LIMIT 1`; if none:
      `SELECT user_id FROM user_code_aliases WHERE code = $userCode LIMIT 1`, then the
      same user columns by `id`; none -> null;
   2. `SELECT * FROM folders WHERE owner_id = $owner.id AND slug = $slug LIMIT 1` (no
      ORDER BY); none, or `is_public` false -> null;
   3. items: `SELECT id, code, ext, filename, mime_type, size, blurhash, storage_key,
      created_at FROM uploads WHERE folder_id = $folder.id AND published = true AND
      deleted_at IS NULL AND password_hash IS NULL AND (expires_at IS NULL OR
      expires_at > now()) AND (max_views IS NULL OR views < max_views) ORDER BY
      created_at DESC` (no LIMIT, no kind filter, no owner filter).

   null -> **notFound** (`app/a/[userCode]/[slug]/page.tsx:75`). No redirect.
4. Props (`port:pages/a/[userCode]/[slug]/page.tsx:15-28`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `album` | `{name: string; slug: string}` | `folders.name`, `folders.slug` |
   | `owner` | `{name: string \| null; theme: string \| null}` | `user.name`, `user.theme` raw |
   | `ownerCode` | string | `owner.user_code ?? params.userCode` (`:78`, page spelling) |
   | `ownerBase` | string | `userBaseUrl(owner.preferred_domain)` (`:85`) |
   | `background` | `{url, dim} \| null` | `backgroundStyle(owner.user_code, background_key, background_dim)` (`:102`; null when reached through an alias of an owner who has no tag now) |
   | `tiles` | `PublicTileItem[]` | items mapped to the 7 tile fields (`:88-96`) |

   Never sent: `storage_key`, `created_at`, other folder columns, owner id,
   `background_key`, raw `preferred_domain`.
5. Metadata (`:22-65`):
   - not found: `{title: t("public.album.notFoundTitle"), robots: {index: false, follow: false}}`
     (en `Album not found`, pl `Nie znaleziono albumu`) - browser-side only, payload
     per section 5.11 (**verified** `/a/x/y`).
   - found: `title = folder.name`; `ownerName = owner.name || t("public.album.anonymous")`
     (en `Anonymous`, pl `Anonim`); `description = t("public.album.metaDescription",
     {count: items.length, owner: ownerName})` (en `{count, plural, one {# item}
     other {# items}} · an album by {owner}`, pl `{count, plural, one {# element} few
     {# elementy} many {# elementów} other {# elementu}} · album użytkownika {owner}`);
     `url = ownerBase + "/a/" + ownerCode + "/" + folder.slug`; `ogImage` as in 6.22
     with `rawUrl(ownerCode, cover.code, cover.ext, ownerBase)`; the object is 6.22's
     with `openGraph.type: "website"`. Tags as in 6.22 with `<title><folder name> - A</title>`
     and `og:type=website`.
6. Document: 200, `data-theme = resolveTheme(owner.theme)`, owner wallpaper. No side
   effects.

### 6.24 `/s/[code]/unlock` (password gate of a short link)

1. URL `/s/:code/unlock` (also reached on the shortener host as `/:code/unlock`,
   section 5.2); layouts: root. No gate, no session read.
2. Inputs: `params.code` (page spelling). Locale only.
3. In order (`app/s/[code]/unlock/page.tsx:45-62`): `SELECT short_links.id,
   short_links.password_hash, "user".theme, "user".user_code, "user".background_key,
   "user".background_dim FROM short_links LEFT JOIN "user" ON "user".id =
   short_links.owner_id WHERE short_links.code = $code LIMIT 1`; no row ->
   **notFound** (`:60`); `password_hash` empty/NULL ->
   `{redirect: "/s/" + code, status: 307}` (`:62`, relative). Expiry is not checked
   (an expired protected link still shows the gate).
4. Props (`port:pages/s/[code]/unlock/page.tsx:16-23`):

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `code` | string | the stored code looked up (the param) |
   | `ownerTheme` | `string \| null` | `"user".theme` (null without an owner row or with a NULL column) |
   | `background` | `{url, dim} \| null` | `backgroundStyle(user_code, background_key, background_dim)` |

   Never sent: `password_hash`, `target`, `id`, `clicks`, owner id.
5. Metadata (`:17-23`), every outcome:
   `{title: t("public.shortlink.metaTitle"), robots: {index: false, follow: false}}`
   -> `Protected link - A` (pl `Link chroniony - A`); tags
   `[description D, application-name A, robots "noindex, nofollow"]`.
   (**Verified** as the browser-side head of `/s/nonexistent/unlock`; the 404 payload
   uses section 5.11.)
6. Document: 200, `data-theme = resolveTheme(ownerTheme)` (NULL -> `discord`), owner
   wallpaper. No side effects (the cookie `ml_sl_<id>` is set by the
   `verifyShortlinkPassword` action, not by the page).

### 6.25 `/s/[code]/[name]/unlock`

Exactly 6.24 for the stored code `scopedShortCode(params.code, params.name)` =
`code + "/" + name` (`app/s/[code]/[name]/unlock/page.tsx:18-25`, `lib/naming.ts:159-161`),
with the same `generateMetadata` (`:7`). `props.code` is `"<tag>/<name>"`; without a
password the redirect is `/s/<tag>/<name>`. Payload `params`: `{code, name}`.
**Verified**: `/s/nonexistent/foo/unlock` -> 404, same heads as 6.24.

### 6.26 `/[userCode]` (tagless media link `/{code}.{ext}`)

1. URL `/:userCode` (one segment that no static route claims); layouts: root.
2. Inputs: the segment. Resolver `resolveTag(segment)` (`app/[userCode]/page.tsx:36-46`):
   1. empty, or starts with `.` -> null;
   2. `code = decodeURIComponent(segment).split(".")[0]` (a SECOND decode); empty -> null;
   3. `isReservedTag(segment) || isReservedTag(code)` (`lib/naming.ts:88-115`:
      lower-cased membership in RESERVED_TAGS) -> null;
   4. `resolveTaglessOwnerTag(code)` (`lib/data/user-prefs.ts:211-222`):
      `SELECT "user".user_code, "user".skip_tag_in_url FROM uploads INNER JOIN "user" ON
      "user".id = uploads.owner_id WHERE uploads.code = $code AND uploads.deleted_at IS
      NULL LIMIT 1`; no row, `skip_tag_in_url` falsy or `user_code` NULL -> null; else
      `tag = user_code`.

   The page runs this on the encoded spelling (so its second decode restores the
   decoded segment); `generateMetadata` runs it on the decoded spelling (so a decoded
   segment containing `%` throws `URIError`: **verified** `/x%25`, section 5.3).
3. Page: tag null -> **notFound** (`:58`); else the media page (6.27) with
   `{userCode: tag, mediaCode: segment}` (`:59`) - its redirects and notFound apply,
   including `taglessActionRedirect`.
4. Props: exactly 6.27's `MediaPageProps` (`port:pages/[userCode]/page.tsx:11`);
   payload `route` = `"/[userCode]"`, `params` = `{userCode: <segment>}`.
5. Metadata (`:48-53`): tag null -> `{title: "Not found", robots: {index: false,
   follow: false}}` (English literal; browser-side only, **verified** on `/x`); else
   the media page's `generateMetadata` with `{userCode: tag, mediaCode: segment}`.
6. Document: as 6.27 (owner skin and wallpaper).

### 6.27 `/[userCode]/[mediaCode]` (public media viewer)

`P` = `app/[userCode]/[mediaCode]/page.tsx`. Port: `port:pages/[userCode]/[mediaCode]/page.tsx:26-58`.

1. **URL** `/:userCode/:mediaCode` (every two-segment path no static route claims,
   e.g. `/a/x`, `/login/x`); also reached through 6.26. Layouts: root. No gate; the
   viewer relation decides what is shown.
2. **Inputs**
   - Params (section 5.3 spellings). Page: `tag = E1`, `mediaCode = E2` (encoded);
     `code = decodeURIComponent(E2).split(".")[0]` (`lib/media/paths.ts:22-24`), so the
     page effectively decodes once. `generateMetadata` gets the decoded `D1`, `D2` and
     decodes `D2` AGAIN: `%` not followed by two hex digits throws `URIError`, `%XX`
     looks up a different code than the page (crafted URLs only; generated tags and
     codes use `[A-Za-z0-9._-]`, `lib/naming.ts:128-134,287-297`). The extension in
     the URL is decorative (`abc`, `abc.png`, `abc.tar.gz` are the same row).
   - Headers: `user-agent` (`?? ""`, `P:225`), `host` (`P:393`), `x-api-key`,
     `authorization` (`lib/api-token.ts:49-58`: `x-api-key` trimmed non-empty wins,
     else `authorization` trimmed whose lower-case starts with `"bearer "` ->
     `slice(7).trim()`, empty -> none).
   - Cookies: presence of ANY cookie whose name contains `session_token`
     (`lib/media/serve.ts:167-168`), the Better Auth session, `ml_unlock_<uploads.id>`,
     `ml_dpw_<owner_id>` (`P:255-259`, `lib/password.ts:175-198`).
   - Query: read only for the tagless-action redirect (step 3.1); `generateMetadata`
     reads none. On the 6.26 path the query is `{}`.
   - `generateMetadata` reads no cookie and no header: its result is the same for
     every viewer.
3. **Evaluation order** (page, `P:201-477`):
   1. `found = getMedia(tag, mediaCode)` (`lib/media/serve.ts:49-113`): `SELECT * FROM
      uploads WHERE code = $code AND deleted_at IS NULL LIMIT 1`; null when there is
      no row, `owner_id` is NULL, the owner row (`id, user_code, theme,
      preferred_domain, background_key, background_dim`) is missing, `tag !==
      owner.user_code` and no `user_code_aliases` row has `code = tag` and
      `user_id = owner.id`, `expires_at <= now`, or `max_views IS NOT NULL AND
      views >= max_views`. Banned owners and `hide_uploads` are not checked.
      **null** -> `taglessActionRedirect(tag, mediaCode, query)` (`P:165,180-199`):
      when `mediaCode` matches `/^(raw(\.[a-z0-9]+)?|download|convert|timeline\.(vtt|jpg))$/i`
      and `resolveTaglessOwnerTag(decodeURIComponent(tag).split(".")[0])` (6.26)
      gives `ownerTag` -> `{redirect: "/" + ownerTag + "/" + code + "/" + mediaCode +
      (qs ? "?" + qs : ""), status: 307}` where `qs` = URLSearchParams serialisation
      of the parsed query (repeated keys appended in order; JS object key order, so
      integer-like keys first); otherwise **notFound**.
   2. `{privileged, isOwner} = viewerRelation(owner_id)` (`lib/media/serve.ts:141-183`):
      with an API token: `authenticateApiToken(token, "read")` (`lib/api-token.ts:101-130`;
      unknown/revoked token, unknown scope, banned token owner or a scope without
      `read` -> anonymous, the session is then not consulted; `read`, `write`, `full`
      grant read, `lib/api-scopes.ts:57-62`); without one: no `*session_token*` cookie
      -> anonymous, else `getSession()`. `isOwner = viewer.id === owner_id`,
      `privileged = isOwner || role === "admin"`.
   3. `!published && !privileged` -> **notFound** (`P:217`).
   4. Direct-media bot: `isPreviewBot(ua) && isDirectMediaBot(ua) && published &&
      !password_hash && max_views == null && mediaKind(mime, ext) in {image, video,
      audio}` -> `{redirect: raw, status: 307}`, or `raw + "?w=1200&fit=inside"` for an
      image over 8388608 bytes (`P:232-246`; ABSOLUTE URL on the owner's host;
      privilege is not considered). `DIRECT_MEDIA_BOTS` =
      `/discordbot|telegrambot|slackbot|slack-imgproxy|skypeuripreview|mattermost|synapse|revoltbot|guildedbot/i`;
      `PREVIEW_BOTS` = that plus `whatsapp twitterbot facebookexternalhit facebot
      linkedinbot redditbot embedly iframely vkshare tumblr flipboard bitlybot nuzzel
      bufferbot "quora link preview" showyoubot outbrain googlebot bingbot applebot
      duckduckbot yandexbot petalbot semrushbot ahrefsbot cardyb w3c_validator`
      (case-insensitive substrings, `lib/media/bots.ts:21-42`; an empty UA is no bot).
   5. `password_hash` truthy and not privileged: unlocked when cookie
      `ml_unlock_<id>` verifies for parts `["media", id]`, or (when
      `password_version != null`) `ml_dpw_<owner_id>` verifies for
      `["owner", owner_id, "v" + password_version]`; token `"<exp>.<sig>"`,
      `exp = Number(prefix)` a safe integer > 0 with `exp*1000 > now`,
      `sig = base64url(HMAC-SHA256(AUTH_SECRET, [...parts, String(exp)].map(encodeURIComponent).join("|")))`,
      constant-time compare (`lib/password.ts:175-198,219-254`). Not unlocked -> view
      **`gate`**.
   6. Encrypted upload (normalised mime `application/vnd.modlight.encrypted` AND
      lower-cased ext `mlenc`, `lib/crypto/envelope.ts:151,163,624-632`) -> view
      **`encrypted`** (any viewer, no view counted).
   7. `!privileged && !isPreviewBot(ua)` -> `consumeView(row)`; refused -> **notFound**
      (`P:336`).
   8. View **`media`**.
4. **Props** (`MediaPageProps`, a union on `view`). Notation: `ownerUserCode =
   owner.user_code ?? tag` (`serve.ts:107`), `ownerBase = userBaseUrl(owner.preferred_domain)`
   (`P:219`), `page = publicUrl(ownerUserCode, code, ext, ownerBase)` =
   `ownerBase/ownerUserCode/code[.ext]`, `raw = ownerBase/ownerUserCode/code/raw[.ext]`,
   `dl = ownerBase/ownerUserCode/code/download` (`P:220-222`, `lib/media/urls.ts:42-44,63-65,142-144`),
   `mime = rowMimeType(row)` (`lib/media/paste-type.ts:44-50,62-69`: for `kind = 'paste'`
   the normalised type of `LANG_EXT[language.trim().toLowerCase()]` when that is a text
   payload, else the stored type; files keep the stored string verbatim).

   Common to all views:

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `ownerTheme` | string | `owner.theme ?? "modern"` (`serve.ts:109`): a NULL column becomes `"modern"`, which resolves to `default` (Classic), not `discord` |
   | `ownerBackground` | `{url, dim} \| null` | `backgroundStyle(owner.user_code, owner.background_key, owner.background_dim)` (`serve.ts:110`; current tag, never the URL tag) |
   | `view` | `"gate" \| "encrypted" \| "media"` | step 3 |

   `view: "gate"` (`P:261-277`): `mediaId: string` = `uploads.id`;
   `onDefaultPassword: boolean` = `password_version != null && password_version ===
   getOwnerPasswordVersion(owner_id)` (`lib/data/user-prefs.ts:154-164`: `SELECT
   default_password_hash, default_password_version FROM "user" WHERE id = $owner`;
   no hash -> null, else `version ?? 0`). Nothing else about the file.

   `view: "encrypted"` (`P:299-317`): `rawPath = "/" + ownerUserCode + "/" + code +
   "/raw." + ext` (relative, `rawUrl(..., "")`); `cipherSize = size`;
   `createdAt = created_at.toISOString()` (string); `views`, `downloads` = row values.

   `view: "media"`: `viewer: MediaViewerProps`
   (`port:overrides/components/viewer/MediaViewer.tsx:49-109`), every key present:

   | Field | Type | Source |
   |---|---|---|
   | `filename` | string | `uploads.filename` |
   | `mimeType` | string | `mime` |
   | `ext` | string | `uploads.ext` |
   | `size` | number | `uploads.size` |
   | `views` | number | `uploads.views` as read in step 1 (BEFORE this request's increment) |
   | `downloads` | number | `uploads.downloads` |
   | `createdAt` | **Date** -> `{"$date"}` | `uploads.created_at` |
   | `width`, `height`, `frames` | number \| null | row |
   | `publicUrl` | string | `page` (always the tagged form) |
   | `rawUrl` | string | `raw` |
   | `downloadUrl` | string | `dl` |
   | `copyRaw` | boolean | `!!SELECT copy_raw FROM "user" WHERE id = owner_id`; any error -> false (`P:320-330`) |
   | `canEncode` | boolean | `privileged` |
   | `signInUrl` | string \| null | `!privileged && hostLower && hostLower !== new URL(appUrl).host.toLowerCase()` ? `publicUrl(ownerUserCode, code, ext, config.appUrl)` : null (`P:392-397`; WHATWG `host` includes a non-default port) |
   | `textContent` | string \| null | when `showsSource` (`lib/media/source-preview.ts:43-45`: `isTextPayload(mime, ext)`, i.e. `serveDecision` is inline `text/plain`, `lib/media/mime.ts:206-258`) and `size <= 524288`: the whole object decoded as UTF-8 (invalid bytes -> U+FFFD, BOM kept); else null |
   | `textUnavailable` | `"too-large" \| "unreadable" \| null` | text and `size > 524288` -> `too-large`; storage read error -> `unreadable` |
   | `renderPath` | string \| null | `canRenderInline` (`lib/media/render-ticket.ts:50-55,76-110`, `lib/media/docx-support.ts:18,37-52`: normalised stored mime `text/html`/`application/xhtml+xml` -> html, `image/svg+xml` -> svg, the docx mime or ext `docx` -> docx; size <= 10 MiB for docx, <= 5 MiB otherwise) -> `"/" + ownerUserCode + "/" + code + "/render?t=" + encodeURIComponent(ticket)`, `ticket = id + "." + exp + "." + base64url(HMAC-SHA256(AUTH_SECRET, "render\|" + encodeURIComponent(id) + "\|" + exp))`, `exp = floor(now/1000) + 900` (`render-ticket.ts:24,117-120,137-140`) |
   | `timelineTrack` | string \| null | `mediaKind == "video" && 0 < size <= 536870912` -> `"/" + ownerUserCode + "/" + code + "/timeline.vtt"` (`lib/media/timeline-preview.ts:69,194-201`) |
   | `posterUrl` | string \| null | `mediaKind == "video"` -> `"/" + ownerUserCode + "/" + code + "/raw?w=800"` (no extension, `P:417-420`) |
   | `playback` | `PlaybackDiagnosis \| null` | `isOwner ? diagnosePlayback(row) : null` (`P:432`; an admin who is not the owner gets null) |
   | `playbackRepair` | `{item: FileItem; userCode: string; appUrl: string} \| null` | `playback ? {item: toFileItems([row], new Map())[0], userCode: ownerUserCode, appUrl: ownerBase} : null` (`P:436-442`) |

   - `PlaybackDiagnosis` (`lib/media/playback-health.ts:138-254`): `{faults:
     ("oversized" | "starved")[], edge, bitsPerPixel: number | null,
     starvationFactor: number | null, repairable: boolean, repairEdge: number}`; null
     unless `mediaKind == "video"`; `edge = max(width ?? 0, height ?? 0)`, `<= 0` ->
     null; `oversized` when `edge > 4096`; frames = `frames` column when non-null
     (used only when > 0, rounded) else `round(duration_seconds * fps)` when > 0;
     `bitsPerPixel = size*8 / (w*h*frames)` (null when any is missing); `starved`
     when `0 < bpp < 2/256`; no fault -> null; `starvationFactor = starved ?
     round(0.065/bpp) : null`; `repairable = max(w, h) > 1920`; `repairEdge = 1920`.
   - `toFileItems` (`lib/data/uploads.ts:216-244`): mapping M ("Dashboard helpers")
     PLUS `durationSeconds`, `fps`, with `mimeType = mime` and `folderName = null`.
   - `mediaKind(mime, ext)` (`lib/media/mime.ts:262-273`) on `effectiveType`:
     displayable `image/*` -> image (other images -> other), `video/*`, `audio/*`,
     `text/*`, `application/pdf` -> pdf, `/zip|gzip|tar|rar|7z-compressed/` -> archive,
     else other.

   Never sent: `password_hash`, `password_version`, `delete_token`, `storage_key`,
   `thumb_key`, `checksum`, `owner_id`, `language`, the owner's
   `default_password_hash` or email, unlock tokens, the `background_key`.
   `textContent` and the render ticket exist only after every gate passed.
5. **Side effects of the GET** (page only; `generateMetadata` only reads):
   - API token header present: `UPDATE api_tokens SET last_used_at = now() WHERE
     token_hash = sha256_hex(token) AND revoked_at IS NULL RETURNING user_id, scope`,
     even when the scope is then refused (`lib/api-token.ts:30-32,91-93,107-111`).
   - `consumeView` (`lib/media/serve.ts:197-229`, step 3.7 only): uncapped ->
     `UPDATE uploads SET views = views + 1, last_accessed_at = now WHERE id = $id`,
     fire-and-forget, errors ignored; capped -> the same with `AND (max_views IS NULL
     OR views < max_views) RETURNING id`, awaited, 0 rows -> notFound. No dedupe; no
     count for privileged viewers, preview bots, the gate, the encrypted view or the
     bot redirect.
   - No cookie writes, no audit rows.
   - Upstream runs `generateMetadata` concurrently with the page and both call
     `getMedia`; compute the metadata from the step-1 row (before `consumeView`) so a
     view that exhausts a cap still gets its card (upstream's ordering is not
     deterministic there).
6. **Metadata** (`P:68-127`, `lib/media/embed.ts:141-326`; no translations, English
   literals only). With `getMedia(D1, D2)`:
   - **M0** null -> `{title: "Not found"}` (`P:70-71`) - always paired with a page
     redirect or notFound; the notFound payload uses section 5.11 (**verified** `/x/y`:
     404, server head = `<title>Modlight</title>` + robots `noindex` + Std, RSC head
     `Not found - Modlight` + Std without robots).
   - Found: `hidden = !!password_hash || !published`; `snippet` (`P:87-89,130-153`) =
     when `!hidden && showsSource && size > 0`: the first 8192 bytes (whole file when
     smaller) decoded as UTF-8, any error -> null (a view cap does NOT hide it);
     context: `brand = config.embedBrand`, `appName`, `page`, `raw`,
     `oembedHref = ownerBase + "/oembed?url=" + encodeURIComponent(page) + "&format=json"`,
     `linkInfoHref = ownerBase + "/api/link-info?url=" + encodeURIComponent(page)`,
     `cardOrigin = ownerBase`; media facts `filename, ext, mimeType = mime, size, width,
     height, kind, published, hasPassword = !!password_hash, hasViewCap = max_views != null`.
   - **F1 protected** (`password_hash` set; first): `{title: {absolute: A}, description:
     null, applicationName: null, robots: {index: false, follow: false}}`.
     -> `meta = {title: A, description: null, applicationName: null, robots:
     "noindex, nofollow"}`; head: `<title>A</title>`, the robots tag, the icons.
   - **F2 unlisted** (`!published`): the same with `title.absolute = filename` (not
     elided) -> `{title: <filename>, description: null, applicationName: null,
     robots: "noindex, nofollow"}`. Only
     privileged viewers get a page here; strangers get notFound (section 5.11).
   - **F3 card**: `title = elideMiddle(filename, 100)`, `documentTitle = title + " · " +
     brand` (U+00B7 between spaces); `mediaKind`, `isImage = isDisplayableImage(mime,
     ext)`, `isText = kind == "paste" || mediaKind == "text" || mime.startsWith("text/")`.
     - image: `og.type = "website"`, `twitterCard = "summary_large_image"`,
       `description = dimensionsDescription`, `image = hasViewCap ? none : embedImage(raw, title)`;
     - video: `og.type = "video.other"`, `twitterCard = "summary"`,
       `description = dimensionsDescription`, `video = hasViewCap ? none : {url: raw,
       type: mime (verbatim), width: width ?? 1280, height: height ?? 720}`, no image;
     - everything else: `og.type = "website"`, `twitterCard = "summary_large_image"`,
       `description = isText ? (snippetDescription(snippet) ?? sizeDescription) :
       sizeDescription`, `image = {url: cardUrl, width: 1200, height: 630, type:
       "image/png", alt: longType}` (present even with a view cap).

     Object (`embed.ts:259-325`): `{title: {absolute: documentTitle}, description:
     null, applicationName: null, alternates: {canonical: page, types:
     {"application/json+oembed": oembedHref, "application/json": linkInfoHref}},
     openGraph: {type, title, siteName: brand, url: page, description, images?:
     [{url, secureUrl: url, width, height, type, alt}], videos?: [{url, secureUrl:
     url, type, width, height}]}, twitter: {card, title: {absolute: og.title},
     description: og.description, images: og.image ? [og.image.url] : undefined}}`.

     `meta = {title: documentTitle, description: null, applicationName: null, robots:
     null}`; head tags in Next order, after `<title>`:
     1. `link rel=canonical href=<page>`
     2. `link rel=alternate type=application/json+oembed href=<oembedHref>`
     3. `link rel=alternate type=application/json href=<linkInfoHref>`
     4. `meta property=og:title content=<title>`
     5. `og:description` = description
     6. `og:url` = page
     7. `og:site_name` = brand
     8. with an image: `og:image` = the URL WHATWG-normalised; `og:image:secure_url` =
        the URL verbatim; `og:image:type`; `og:image:width`, `og:image:height` (when
        truthy); `og:image:alt`
     9. with a video: `og:video`, `og:video:secure_url` (both verbatim), `og:video:type`,
        `og:video:width`, `og:video:height`
     10. `og:type` = `website` or `video.other`
     11. `meta name=twitter:card` = `summary_large_image` / `summary`
     12. `twitter:title` = title
     13. `twitter:description` = description
     14. with an image: `twitter:image` = the normalised URL

     No `description`, `application-name`, robots, `og:audio` or twitter player tags
     on any media page. Twitter values are not auto-filled (title, description and card
     are given; the image list mirrors the OG image).
   - Helpers (`embed.ts`): `dimensionsDescription` = `[shortType, w+"x"+h when both
     truthy, humanFileSize(size)].join(" · ")` (`:333-338`); `sizeDescription` =
     `longType + " · " + humanFileSize(size)` (`:341-343`); `shortType` = `ext ?
     ext.toUpperCase() : ((mime.split("/")[1] ?? mime).split("+")[0]).toUpperCase()`
     (`:362-366`); `longType` = `LONG_TYPES[ext.toLowerCase()]` (`:368-397`: pdf "PDF
     document", zip "ZIP archive", rar "RAR archive", 7z "7z archive", tar "TAR
     archive", gz/tgz "GZIP archive", mp3 "MP3 audio", wav "WAV audio", flac "FLAC
     audio", ogg "OGG audio", opus "Opus audio", m4a "M4A audio", txt "Text file", md
     "Markdown document", json "JSON file", csv "CSV spreadsheet", html "HTML
     document", svg "SVG image", doc/docx "Word document", xls/xlsx "Excel
     spreadsheet", ppt/pptx "PowerPoint presentation"), else `shortType + " audio"` /
     `shortType + " archive"` for those kinds, else `shortType` (`:400-407`);
     `snippetDescription` (`:346-359`): remove U+FFFD, map U+0000-001F and U+007F to a
     space, collapse JS `\s+` to one space, trim, empty -> null, longer than 180 UTF-16
     units -> `slice(0, 180).trimEnd() + "..."`; `elideMiddle(name, 100)` (`:486-495`,
     UTF-16 units): unchanged when `length <= 100`; `dot = lastIndexOf(".")`, `ext =
     dot > 0 && length - dot <= 12 ? name.slice(dot) : ""`, `room = 97 - ext.length`;
     `room < 8` -> `name.slice(0, 97) + "..."`; else `head = ceil(room*0.6)`,
     `tail = room - head`, `name.slice(0, head) + "..." + name.slice(length -
     ext.length - tail, length - ext.length) + ext`; `humanFileSize`
     (`lib/format.ts:2-12`): non-finite or <= 0 -> `"0 B"`, divide by 1024 while
     `>= 1024` (B KB MB GB TB PB), bytes printed as the integer, other units
     `toFixed(1)` (JS rounding: 1280 B -> `1.3 KB`); `embedImage` (`:421-475`):
     `declared = effectiveType(mime, ext)`; size > 33554432 or (width <= 1200 and size
     <= 8388608) -> `{url: raw, width?, height?, type: declared, alt}` (width/height
     omitted when NULL); else `url = raw + "?w=1200&fit=inside"`, `type =
     "image/webp"`, and when `width > 1200 && height > 0` width 1200, height
     `max(1, round(height*1200/width))`, otherwise the row's width/height.
   - Card URL (`lib/media/card.ts:120-202`): `cardUrl = ownerBase.replace(/\/+$/, "") +
     "/embed/v1/" + category + "-" + token + ".png"`; category, first match: paste ->
     `text`; `EXT_CATEGORY[ext.toLowerCase()]` (`:84-102`); mediaKind audio ->
     `audio`, archive -> `archive`, pdf -> `document`, text -> `text`; lower-cased
     mime starting `text/` -> `text`, `image/` -> `image`; ending `+json`/`+xml` or
     `application/json` or containing `javascript` -> `code`; containing
     `spreadsheet`/`ms-excel` -> `sheet`; containing `word`/`presentation`/`powerpoint`
     -> `document`; else `binary`. Token (`:177-181`): `ext.toUpperCase()` stripped to
     `[A-Z0-9]`, first 8 characters, kept when its lower-case is a key of
     `EXT_CATEGORY` (any category), else the category word (`CODE ARCHIVE DOCUMENT
     SHEET AUDIO TEXT IMAGE FILE`). (A paste in, say, `ts` gets `text-TS.png`, which
     the `/embed` route refuses: upstream behaviour.)
   - Variant matrix: displayable image (static or animated) -> image card; capped
     image -> no `og:image`/`twitter:image`, card still `summary_large_image`; any
     `video/*` effective type -> `video.other`, `og:video` unless capped, twitter
     `summary`; audio -> type card `audio-<TOKEN>`; pdf -> `document-PDF`; text, code,
     paste -> type card, snippet description when `isText`; non-displayable images
     (svg, heic, psd) -> `image-<TOKEN>` card; encrypted `.mlenc` -> `binary-FILE`
     card; password -> F1; unpublished -> F2; missing/trashed/expired/burned/orphan/
     wrong tag -> M0 + notFound.

   Worked example (brand `example`, `ownerBase = https://h.example.com`, tag `alicja`,
   code `kalit2`, `screenshot.png`, 1920x1080, 245760 B, published, no cap):
   `<title>screenshot.png · example</title>`, canonical
   `https://h.example.com/alicja/kalit2.png`, oEmbed
   `https://h.example.com/oembed?url=https%3A%2F%2Fh.example.com%2Falicja%2Fkalit2.png&format=json`,
   link-info `https://h.example.com/api/link-info?url=https%3A%2F%2Fh.example.com%2Falicja%2Fkalit2.png`,
   `og:description` `PNG · 1920x1080 · 240.0 KB`, `og:image`
   `https://h.example.com/alicja/kalit2/raw.png?w=1200&fit=inside` (type `image/webp`,
   1200x675, alt `screenshot.png`), `og:type` `website`, `twitter:card`
   `summary_large_image`, `twitter:image` = the same URL.
7. **Document**: 200 for the three views; 307 + absolute `Location` for the bot
   redirect (bots read only the document, so this must happen on document loads);
   307 relative for the tagless-action redirect; 404 per section 5.11.
   `data-theme = resolveTheme(ownerTheme)` and the owner's wallpaper in all three views
   (`P:270,302,446`); the applier does not persist (`ml_skin` is never written here).

---

## 7. Server actions: `POST /_rpc`

Every exported function of upstream `app/actions/*.ts` (18 modules, 94
actions) is a stub in `server/web/src/rpc/actions/<module>.ts` with the same
name, parameters and return type. `server/web/src/rpc/actions.manifest.json`
lists all 94 as data (RPC path, parameter and return types as TypeScript text,
upstream line), for checking the server's router against it.

### 7.1 Protocol

Request (`server/web/src/runtime/rpc.ts`):

```
POST /_rpc/<module>/<export>          module = file name without .ts ("uploads", "shortlink-unlock")
Content-Type: application/json
X-Modlight-Rpc: 1
Origin: <the page's origin>            (sent by the browser on every POST)

["first argument", {"second": {"$date": "2026-10-18T12:00:00.000Z"}}]
```

- The body is the tagged JSON array of the arguments **the caller passed**
  (`arguments`, not the declared parameters): an omitted optional argument is
  absent, and the server applies upstream's default parameter value (listed per
  action below). `uploads.emptyTrash()` sends `[]`, `emptyTrash("bobby")` sends
  `["bobby"]`.
- A call whose single argument is a `FormData` goes as `multipart/form-data`
  with `X-Modlight-Rpc: form` (no upstream action takes one today).

Before the action runs (`server/src/rpc/mod.rs`, as Next's action handler
does), a refusal answers a non-200 status with the JSON body
`{"error": {"message": "..."}}`, which the SPA rejects like an error envelope:

| Refusal | Status | `message` |
|---|---|---|
| `X-Modlight-Rpc` missing, or `Origin` naming another host than the request's (PORT.md section 7) | 403 | `Invalid Server Actions request.` |
| unknown module or export | 404 | `Server action not found.` |
| body over **1 MiB** (Next's default `serverActions.bodySizeLimit`) | 413 | `Body exceeded 1 MB limit.` |
| body unreadable or not a JSON array | 400 | `Invalid RPC request.` |

Upstream's wire differs for these, but its caller also just sees a rejected
promise (an unknown action rejects with "Server Action ... was not found on the
server", the others with the masked error). Next also refuses more than 1000
arguments; the skeleton does not check that yet (no action declares more than 3).
Then the session is resolved, and refreshed or expired, exactly as Better Auth
does for any server-side call (7.2.3).

Response: HTTP `200`, `Content-Type: application/json`,
`Cache-Control: private, no-cache, no-store, max-age=0, must-revalidate`, one
tagged JSON envelope, plus any `Set-Cookie`:

| Upstream action | Envelope |
|---|---|
| returned a value | `{"ok": <value>}`; a void action: `{"ok": {"$undefined": 1}}` |
| `redirect(url)` (only via `requireUser`/`requireAdmin`) | `{"redirect": "<url>"}` (type `push`; `"redirectType": "replace"` would make it a replace) |
| `notFound()` (no action calls it) | `{"notFound": true}` |
| threw | production: `{"error": {"message": "<masked text, section 4.4>", "digest": "<id>"}}`; development: the real message, no digest (as `next dev`) |

and `"revalidate": true` added to any of them when either

- a `revalidatePath`/`revalidateTag` call ran, even if the action then threw or
  redirected (Next sets the header in a `finally`), or
- the response sets or deletes **any** cookie: Next 16 counts modified cookies
  as a revalidation (`addRevalidationHeader` in `action-handler.js`) and its
  client refreshes the page. That includes the unlock and sign-in cookies and
  Better Auth's session-cookie refresh or expiry (7.2.3). The skeleton sets
  `revalidate` only for `revalidatePath`/`revalidateTag` so far (open question
  12.3).

What the browser does with it (matching Next 16's
`router-reducer/reducers/server-action-reducer.js`):

- `ok`: resolves with the value, after refetching the current page when
  `revalidate` is set;
- `redirect`: navigates (client-side to an SPA page, a document load otherwise)
  and rejects the call with Next's redirect error (digest
  `NEXT_REDIRECT;push;<url>;307;`, marked `handled`, so it never navigates twice);
- `error`: rejects with `Error(message)` carrying `.digest`;
- `notFound`: rejects with Next's not-found error;
- a refusal (the table above): rejects with `Error(message)`;
- a response that is not JSON (a proxy's error page): rejects with
  `Error("An unexpected response was received from the server.")`, a network
  failure with `TypeError("Failed to fetch")`, as Next does.

The rest of this section is the per-action work order: conventions (7.2), a
summary table (7.3), every action (7.4) and the quirks to confirm (7.5).

### 7.2 Conventions (apply to every action below)

#### 7.2.1 Wire

- Request: `POST /_rpc/<module>/<export>`. The body is a tagged JSON array holding the arguments that were actually passed. An argument that was left out is absent from the array, and the server applies the upstream default (each default is listed in its action). No action takes a single `FormData`, so none of the 94 uses the multipart (`X-Modlight-Rpc: form`) variant.
- Response: always HTTP 200 with a JSON envelope.

| Upstream outcome | Envelope |
|---|---|
| returned a value | `{"ok": <tagged JSON>}` |
| `redirect(url)` | `{"redirect": url}` |
| `notFound()` | `{"notFound": true}` |
| threw | `{"error": {"message": <masked>, "digest": "..."}}` |
| `revalidatePath`/`revalidateTag` ran, **or the response sets or deletes any cookie** (section 7.1) | `"revalidate": true` is added to whichever of the above |

- Masked errors: Next masks the message of an error thrown from a production action. In the blocks below, `Error("X") [masked]` means upstream throws message X, the message is logged on the server, and the client receives Next's generic text (`MASKED_SERVER_ERROR` in `server/web/src/runtime/errors.ts`, section 4.4) plus a digest.
- `notFound()` is called by **no** action, so nothing produces `{"notFound": true}`.
- `redirect()` is reached **only** through `requireUser`/`requireAdmin` (no action redirects on its own). Inside an action, Next's default redirect type is `push`, so `redirectType` may be omitted. The URLs are exactly `/login` and `/dashboard`, with no query string.
- Void actions resolve with `undefined`: answer `{"ok":{"$undefined":1}}` (decided in 7.1; the stub returns `envelope.ok`, so `{}` would also work, but differential tests compare the former).
- Dates in results: only three actions return real `Date` objects, which must be tagged as `{"$date": iso}`:
  - `folders.createFolder`: `createdAt`
  - `shortlinks.listShortLinks`: `lastUsedAt`, `expiresAt`, `createdAt`
  - `tokens.myTokens`: `lastUsedAt`, `revokedAt`, `createdAt`

  Every other timestamp in a result is already an ISO **string** upstream.
- `undefined`-valued properties: React Flight keeps the key, so they must travel as `{"$undefined":1}`. In arguments this matters for `account.updatePreferences`, which tests `"locale" in input`. In results it occurs in:
  - `unlock.verifyMediaPassword`: `remembered`
  - `convert.*`: `note`
  - `admin.createAccount`: `detail`
  - `mail.sendTestMail`: `smtpCode`, `serverSays`
- `bigint` columns are `mode: "number"` in drizzle, so they are plain JSON numbers.

#### 7.2.2 Auth labels

| Label | Upstream | No session | Wrong role |
|---|---|---|---|
| `requireUser` | `lib/auth-helpers.ts:requireUser` | `{"redirect":"/login"}` | - |
| `requireAdmin` | `requireUser`, then `session.user.role !== "admin"` | `{"redirect":"/login"}` | `{"redirect":"/dashboard"}` |
| `session-or-throw` | `getSession()`, then `if (!session) throw new Error("Unauthorized")` | error envelope (masked), **not** a redirect | - |
| `session-or-data` | `getSession()`, then returns a refusal value | the value given in the block | - |
| `public` | no session lookup | - | - |

How the session is resolved:

- Sessions come from better-auth `auth.api.getSession({ headers })`, which reads the signed cookie `better-auth.session_token` (or `__Secure-better-auth.session_token` when APP_URL is https).
- No cookie cache is configured, so `session.user` is read from the DB on every call. It carries `id`, `email`, `name`, `role`, `banned`, `emailVerified`, `twoFactorEnabled` and the additionalFields `userCode`, `usedQuota`, `maxQuota`, `hideUploads`, `copyRaw`, `theme` and `locale`.
- Several actions use these values directly, for example `createPaste`, which checks the quota.

#### 7.2.3 Session-cookie side effects of every non-public action

`lib/auth.ts` installs `nextCookies()`, which copies every `Set-Cookie` produced by a server-side better-auth call onto the action's response. `getSession` (better-auth `api/routes/session.mjs`) behaves as follows:

- No session cookie, or a cookie with a bad signature: returns null and writes no cookie.
- A validly signed token whose row is missing or expired: expires the `session_token`, `session_data` and `dont_remember` cookies and deletes an expired row.
- A valid session whose expiry was last set at least `updateAge` (1 day) ago, i.e. `expires_at - 7d + 1d <= now`, which means 6 days or less remain: `UPDATE session SET expires_at = now+7d, updated_at` and re-issues the `session_token` cookie (Max-Age = seconds until the new `expires_at`, about 604800). Note that the upstream comment in lib/data/sessions.ts ("last `updateAge` of its life") describes this differently; the better-auth code is what runs.

The same applies to every other better-auth call made with the request headers (`listSessions`, `revokeSession`, `setRole`, `banUser`, `createUser`, `removeUser`, ...), because they resolve the session the same way.

The "Cookies" lines below list only what an action does **on top of** this.

Next 16 detail: an action that sets or deletes **any** cookie makes Next send `x-action-revalidated` (`addRevalidationHeader` in `next/dist/server/app-render/action-handler.js` counts modified cookies), and the Next client then refreshes the current route even when no `revalidatePath` ran. The envelope mirrors that: `"revalidate": true` whenever the response carries a `Set-Cookie`, the session-cookie maintenance above included (7.1).

#### 7.2.4 Cookie serialization (Next `ResponseCookies`)

- `cookies().set(name, value, {path, maxAge, sameSite, httpOnly, secure})` emits:
  `name=<encodeURIComponent(value)>; Path=/; Expires=<now+maxAge, UTC>; Max-Age=<n>[; Secure][; HttpOnly]; SameSite=lax`
- `cookies().delete(name)` emits:
  `name=; Path=/; Expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT`

#### 7.2.5 Revalidation

- `"revalidate": true` must be set whenever a `revalidatePath` call **ran**, including when the action later threw or redirected (Next sets the header in a `finally`), and whenever the response sets or deletes a cookie (7.2.3).
- A `revalidatePath` written after a call that threw did **not** run.
- Paths are listed for traceability. `(L)` marks `revalidatePath(path, "layout")`. No action uses `revalidateTag`.

#### 7.2.6 Common failure modes (not repeated per action)

- **No runtime type checks** unless a block says so. A wrong JSON type typically ends in a `TypeError` (for example `tag.trim()` on a number) or a Postgres error, and both become the error envelope.
- **uuid columns**: `uploads.id`, `uploads.folder_id`, `folders.id`, `api_tokens.id`, `short_links.id` and `user.default_folder_id` are uuid. Where an action passes a caller string to them unchecked, a non-uuid makes Postgres raise `22P02` and the action ends in the error envelope. Each block says whether its ids are guarded. `user.id` is `text` and never errors.
- Any uncaught DB or storage exception becomes the error envelope.
- Next refuses, before the action runs, request bodies over **1 MiB** (`serverActions.bodySizeLimit` is not configured upstream, so the default applies) and calls with more than 1000 arguments. Both become the error envelope (masked; Next's own messages are "Body exceeded 1mb limit..." and "Server Action arguments list is too long (n)...").

#### 7.2.7 Shared mechanics

**Audit** (`lib/audit.ts:recordAudit(actorId, action, detail)`)

- Writes `INSERT INTO audit_log (user_id, action, detail, ip)`.
  - `id`: uuid default.
  - `created_at`: default now().
  - `detail`: jsonb, where `undefined`-valued keys are dropped.
  - `ip`: `clientIp(headers)`.
- Runs after the change it records. An exception inside it is swallowed (`console.error`) and never fails the action.

**Client IP** (`lib/password.ts:clientIp`)

- `X-Real-IP`, trimmed, else `"unknown"`.
- PORT.md 12.7: in the port, honour it only when `TRUST_PROXY` is on.

**Rate limiter** (`lib/password.ts:checkRateLimit(key, max = 5, windowMs = 15 min)`)

- Per process, in memory, with a fixed window per key.
- The first hit creates `{count: 1, resetAt: now+window}`. Every later hit increments `count`, refused hits included, and the call is refused while `count > max`.
- `resetRateLimit(key)` deletes the bucket.
- Above 5,000 buckets, expired ones are swept at most every 30 s. Above 20,000, the oldest entries are evicted.
- Nothing is persisted.

**Password formats**

- **(F)** file, short-link and default-file passwords (`lib/password.ts:hashPassword`):
  - Format: `scrypt$<base64url 16-byte salt>$<base64url 32-byte key>`.
  - Node `crypto.scrypt` defaults: N=16384, r=8, p=1.
  - Verified in constant time.
- **(BA)** account passwords (better-auth `hashPassword`, `@better-auth/utils/password`):
  - Format: `<32 hex chars salt>:<128 hex chars key>`.
  - `scrypt(password.normalize("NFKC"), <the salt hex STRING as bytes>, 64, N=16384, r=16, p=1)`.
  - Legacy bcrypt `$2a/$2b/$2y` is verify-only (`lib/auth.ts`).

**Secret box** (`lib/secret-box.ts`; SMTP password and registration key)

- Envelope: `v1.<iv b64url, 12 bytes>.<tag b64url, 16 bytes>.<ciphertext b64url>`, AES-256-GCM.
- Key: HKDF-SHA256 with:
  - IKM: `SETTINGS_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (base64 of exactly 32 bytes) or else the AUTH secret as UTF-8.
  - salt: `"modlight/secret-box/2026"`.
  - info: `"modlight:<purpose>:v1"`, 32 bytes.
- Purposes: `smtp` and `registration-key`.

**Mail**

- `lib/mail.ts:sendMail` sends through nodemailer with:
  - From: `"<fromName>" <from>`.
  - The logo attachment.
  - Timeouts: connect 10 s, greeting 10 s, socket 20 s.
- It throws `MailNotConfiguredError` in production when mail is not ready. In development it logs the message and returns.
- Errors are redacted before they are rethrown.
- `mailReady()` reads a per-process settings snapshot that is refreshed every 30 s (`lib/mail-settings.ts`).
- `pickMailLocale(pref, acceptLanguage)`: `pref` `"pl"` or `"en"` wins. Otherwise `/(?:^|[,\s])pl\b/i` on Accept-Language gives `"pl"`, else `"en"`.

**Unlock tokens** (`lib/password.ts`)

- Value: `<exp unix seconds>.<base64url HMAC-SHA256(AUTH secret, parts...|exp, each part encodeURIComponent'd, joined by "|")>`.
- Parts:
  - `["media", uploadId]`, TTL 86400
  - `["shortlink", linkId]`, TTL 3600
  - `["owner", ownerId, "v<version>"]`, TTL 2592000
- Cookie names: `ml_unlock_<uploadId>`, `ml_sl_<linkId>`, `ml_dpw_<ownerId>`.
- Cookie attributes: `HttpOnly; Path=/; SameSite=lax; Secure` (Secure only when `NODE_ENV=production`).

**Clock and updated_at**

- Timestamps written as `new Date()` use the app clock.
- drizzle `$onUpdate` sets `updated_at = new Date()` on **every** UPDATE of `user`, `session`, `account` and `verification`, even when the code does not name the column.
- `uploads.updated_at` changes only where a block says so.

#### 7.2.8 Request-header dependencies

**X-Real-IP**

- Feeds `audit_log.ip` for every action that writes an audit row.
- Used in rate-limit keys:
  - `authorize.approveDesktopClient`
  - `invite.acceptInvitation`
  - `invite.resendVerification`
  - `login-identifier.signInWithIdentifier`
  - `shortlink-unlock.verifyShortlinkPassword`
  - `unlock.verifyMediaPassword`
- Becomes `session.ip_address` on sign-in (`acceptInvitation`, `signInWithIdentifier`). better-auth is configured with `ipAddressHeaders: ["x-real-ip"]`.

**User-Agent**

- Becomes `session.user_agent` on sign-in (`acceptInvitation`, `signInWithIdentifier`).

**Accept-Language** (mail locale fallback)

- `admin.updateUserAccount`: notification mails, when the target's own `locale` is not pl/en.
- `admin.createAccount`: invitation mail, which always uses the admin's Accept-Language.
- `admin.resendInvite`: fallback after the target's locale.
- `mail.sendTestMail`: fallback after the admin's locale.
- better-auth verification mails sent through `auth.api.*` get no `Request`, so they use only `user.locale` (pl/en) and otherwise `"en"`. This affects `sendVerificationLink`, `resendVerification`, and `signInWithIdentifier` / `acceptInvitation` on an unverified account.

**Cookie**

- Read by every session-based action (7.2.2).
- `signInWithIdentifier` and `acceptInvitation` also read `better-auth.trust_device`.
- `admin.sendVerificationLink` and `invite.resendVerification` strip `Cookie` and `Authorization` before calling better-auth (`headersWithoutSession`).

**Origin / Host**

- Not read by any action. Only the transport-level CSRF rule applies (PORT.md 7: Origin must equal the request's own origin, else 403).
- better-auth's own origin checks are skipped for server-side calls because no `Request` is passed.

### 7.4 Per-action contract

Reading the blocks: "Revalidate" lists the `revalidatePath` calls an action
makes. Per 7.1 the envelope **also** carries `"revalidate": true` whenever the
response sets or deletes a cookie, so every action whose "Cookies" line names a
cookie (and any action during which the session cookie was refreshed or
expired, 7.2.3) revalidates even where its "Revalidate" line says none.

#### Module `account` (app/actions/account.ts)

Shared types (from the stub):

```ts
type TagIssue = "too-short" | "too-long" | "charset" | "reserved" | "taken" | "retired" | "error";
type TagCheckResult = { status: "ok" } | { status: "current" } | { status: "unavailable"; reason: TagIssue };
```

`checkTag(tag)` (lib/naming.ts), used by #2, #3, #17:

- length below 2 gives `too-short`; above 32 gives `too-long` (UTF-16 code units).
- Not matching `/^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9_-]*$/` gives `charset`.
- In `RESERVED_TAGS` (lib/naming.ts, compared case-insensitively) gives `reserved`.
- Tag ownership comparisons in SQL are case-sensitive equality on `user.user_code` and `user_code_aliases.code`.

##### account.updatePreferences
- Signature: `updatePreferences(input: { name?: string; hideUploads?: boolean; copyRaw?: boolean; theme?: string; locale?: string | null }): Promise<PreferencesResult>`, with `PreferencesResult = { theme: string; locale: string | null }`.
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: builds a patch for the caller's `user` row from these keys:
  - `name`: when a string with a non-blank trim, the trimmed value cut to its first 120 chars.
  - `hideUploads` / `copyRaw`: only when booleans.
  - `theme`: only when one of the 14 ids in `lib/themes.ts` THEMES (`default, glass, synthwave, terminal, paper, win98, nebula, grimoire, cartoon, mono, deco, riso, discord, spotify`).
  - `locale`: whenever the key is **present** (`"locale" in input`, so `{locale: undefined}` counts). The value is `"pl"`/`"en"` as given, anything else becomes `null`.

  An empty patch returns `{theme: resolveTheme(session.user.theme), locale: session.user.locale ?? null}` with no write, no cookie and no revalidate. Otherwise it runs `UPDATE "user" SET <patch>, updated_at WHERE id = me RETURNING theme, locale`.
- Returns: `{ theme: resolveTheme(row.theme), locale: row.locale ?? null }`. `resolveTheme` keeps a valid id, maps `"modern"` to `"default"`, and maps anything else to `"discord"`.
- Errors: no row returned gives `Error("Preferences could not be saved")` [masked].
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: `revalidatePath("/", "layout")` after a write.
- Cookies:
  - When `theme` was in the patch: `ml_skin=<resolved theme>; Path=/; Max-Age=31536000; SameSite=lax`. Not HttpOnly, not Secure.
  - When the `locale` key was present and the value is pl/en: `ml_locale=<v>; Path=/; Max-Age=31536000; SameSite=lax`. Otherwise `ml_locale` is deleted.
- Upstream: app/actions/account.ts:45 -> lib/themes.ts:isThemeId/resolveTheme, lib/theme-cookies.ts (SKIN_COOKIE, THEME_COOKIE_MAX_AGE).

##### account.checkTagAvailability
- Signature: `checkTagAvailability(tag: string): Promise<TagCheckResult>`
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: read-only. `clean = tag.trim()`, then:
  - `checkTag(clean)`.
  - If `clean` equals `session.user.userCode` (case-sensitive): `current`.
  - `SELECT id FROM "user" WHERE user_code = clean LIMIT 1` owned by another account: `taken`.
  - `SELECT user_id FROM user_code_aliases WHERE code = clean LIMIT 1` owned by another account: `retired`.
- Returns: `{status:"ok"}` | `{status:"current"}` | `{status:"unavailable", reason}`, where reason is a checkTag reason, `taken` or `retired`.
- Errors: a non-string `tag` gives a TypeError [masked]; otherwise none of its own.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: none. Cookies: none.
- Upstream: account.ts:126 -> lib/naming.ts:checkTag, account.ts:tagConflict (private).

##### account.updateTag
- Signature: `updateTag(tag: string): Promise<{ ok: true; tag: string } | { ok: false; reason: TagIssue }>`
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: `clean = tag.trim()`. A checkTag failure returns `{ok:false, reason}`. A `clean` equal to the current tag returns `{ok:true, tag}` without writing. Otherwise one transaction:
  1. If another user has `user_code = clean`: roll back, `taken`.
  2. If an alias `code = clean` belongs to another user: roll back, `retired`.
  3. If the caller has a current tag: `INSERT INTO user_code_aliases (code, user_id) VALUES (current, me) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (`created_at` defaults to now()).
  4. `UPDATE "user" SET user_code = clean`.

  Any other exception, for example a unique-index race on `user_user_code_idx`, returns `{ok:false, reason:"error"}`.
- Returns: `{ok:true, tag: clean}` | `{ok:false, reason}`.
- Errors: a non-string `tag` gives a TypeError [masked]. Exceptions inside the transaction are returned as `reason:"error"`, not thrown.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: `/dashboard/profile` and `/dashboard`, only after the transaction committed.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:166 -> lib/naming.ts:checkTag.

##### account.updateSkipTagInUrl
- Signature: `updateSkipTagInUrl(enabled: boolean): Promise<void>`
- Auth: requireAdmin. It writes the **caller's own** row.
- Does: `UPDATE "user" SET skip_tag_in_url = enabled WHERE id = me`.
- Returns: undefined.
- Errors: none of its own; the common failure modes in 7.2.6 apply.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` without a session, `{"redirect":"/dashboard"}` for a non-admin (requireAdmin). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: `/dashboard/profile`, `/dashboard` (always).
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:231.

##### account.setAllowRootShortLinks
- Signature: `setAllowRootShortLinks(userId: string, allowed: boolean): Promise<void>`
- Auth: requireAdmin.
- Does: `UPDATE "user" SET allow_root_short_links = allowed WHERE id = userId`. There is no existence check, so an unknown id is a silent no-op.
- Returns: undefined.
- Errors: none of its own; the common failure modes in 7.2.6 apply.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` without a session, `{"redirect":"/dashboard"}` for a non-admin (requireAdmin). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: `/dashboard/admin` (always).
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:242.

##### account.updateDefaultFolder
- Signature: `updateDefaultFolder(folderId: string | null): Promise<{ ok: boolean }>`
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: for a truthy `folderId`, runs `SELECT id FROM folders WHERE id = folderId AND owner_id = me`. No row returns `{ok:false}` with no write. Then `UPDATE "user" SET default_folder_id = folderId` (null clears).
- Returns: `{ok:true}` | `{ok:false}`.
- Errors:
  - A non-uuid `folderId` gives a Postgres error [masked].
  - `""` skips the ownership check and then fails the same way in the UPDATE.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: `/dashboard/profile` when ok.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:255.

##### account.myUrlPrefs
- Signature: `myUrlPrefs(): Promise<UrlPrefs>`, with `UrlPrefs = { scheme: NamingScheme; pattern: string | null; wordCount: number; randomLength: number; preferredDomain: string | null; baseUrl: string }`.
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: reads the caller's `naming_scheme, naming_pattern, naming_word_count, naming_random_length, preferred_domain`. Normalisation:
  - `scheme`: the stored value when it is in `NAMING_SCHEMES` (`random, words, random-chars, filename, filename-random, date-filename, custom`), else `"random"`.
  - `wordCount`: rounded and clamped to 2..4; null or non-finite gives 2.
  - `randomLength`: clamped to 5..32; null gives 6.
  - `preferredDomain`: the raw column.
  - `baseUrl = userBaseUrl(preferredDomain)`: the matching entry of `config.appDomains` (compared case-insensitively, trailing `/` ignored) in the config's spelling, else `config.appUrl`.
- Returns: `UrlPrefs`, normalised as above.
- Errors: none of its own; the common failure modes in 7.2.6 apply.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: none.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:294 -> lib/naming.ts:clampWordCount/clampRandomLength, lib/media/urls.ts:userBaseUrl.

##### account.updateNaming
- Signature: `updateNaming(input: UpdateNamingInput): Promise<void>`, with `UpdateNamingInput = { scheme: string; pattern?: string | null; wordCount?: number | null; randomLength?: number | null }`.
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: `scheme` must be in `NAMING_SCHEMES`. `clean = (pattern ?? "").trim().slice(0,120) || null`. Then:
  `UPDATE "user" SET naming_scheme = scheme, naming_pattern = (scheme == "custom" ? clean : null), naming_word_count = clampWordCount(wordCount), naming_random_length = clampRandomLength(randomLength)`.
  Both numbers are always written; absent or null becomes 2 / 6.
- Returns: undefined.
- Errors (all masked):
  - `Error("Unknown naming scheme")`.
  - `Error("A custom pattern is required for the custom scheme")` when the scheme is custom and `clean` is null.
  - `Error("That pattern does not produce a usable name")`. This is unreachable in practice: `buildCodeBase` falls back to `humanCode(10)` when the expansion is empty.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: `/dashboard/profile`, `/dashboard` after the write.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:338 -> lib/naming.ts:isNamingScheme/buildCodeBase/clampWordCount/clampRandomLength.

##### account.updatePreferredDomain
- Signature: `updatePreferredDomain(domain: string | null): Promise<{ ok: boolean; baseUrl: string }>`
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: `clean = domain?.trim().replace(/\/+$/, "") || null`. A non-null value that is not in `config.appDomains` (case-insensitive) returns `{ok:false, baseUrl: config.appUrl}` with no write. Otherwise `UPDATE "user" SET preferred_domain = clean`, stored exactly as typed after trim and slash strip, with case preserved.
- Returns: `{ok:true, baseUrl: userBaseUrl(clean)}`, which is the config's spelling, or `config.appUrl` for null.
- Errors: a `domain` that is neither a string nor null/undefined gives a TypeError [masked].
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: `revalidatePath("/", "layout")` when ok.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:379 -> lib/media/urls.ts:isKnownDomain/userBaseUrl.

##### account.myDefaultFilePassword
- Signature: `myDefaultFilePassword(): Promise<DefaultPasswordState>`, with `DefaultPasswordState = { enabled: boolean; affected: number }`.
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does:
  - `enabled = user.default_password_hash IS NOT NULL`.
  - `affected = count(*) FROM uploads WHERE owner_id = me AND password_version IS NOT NULL`, trashed rows included.
  - Never returns the hash.
- Returns: `{enabled, affected}`.
- Errors: none of its own; the common failure modes in 7.2.6 apply.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: none.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:421 -> lib/data/user-prefs.ts:getDefaultPasswordPrefs/countFilesOnDefaultPassword.

##### account.updateDefaultFilePassword
- Signature: `updateDefaultFilePassword(password: string | null, applyToExisting?: boolean): Promise<DefaultPasswordResult>`. The default is `applyToExisting = false`. `DefaultPasswordResult = { ok: true; enabled: boolean; updated: number } | { ok: false; reason: "too-short" }`.
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: a non-string password becomes `""`. A length of 1..3 (UTF-16 units) returns `{ok:false, reason:"too-short"}`. A non-empty password is hashed in format (F); empty means null. Then one transaction:
  1. `UPDATE "user" SET default_password_hash = <hash|null>, default_password_version = coalesce(default_password_version,0)+1 WHERE id = me RETURNING default_password_version`. The version is bumped on set, replace **and** clear.
  2. Only if `applyToExisting` is truthy: `UPDATE uploads SET password_hash = <hash|null>, password_version = (hash null ? NULL : new version) WHERE owner_id = me AND password_version IS NOT NULL RETURNING id`. Trashed rows are included and `uploads.updated_at` is untouched. `updated` is the row count.
- Returns: `{ok:true, enabled: hash !== null, updated}` (`updated` is 0 without applyToExisting).
- Errors: no user row gives `Error("Account not found")` [masked] and the transaction is rolled back.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: `/dashboard/profile`, `/dashboard` when ok.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:464 -> lib/password.ts:hashPassword.

##### account.myBackground
- Signature: `myBackground(): Promise<BackgroundState>`, with `BackgroundState = { url: string | null; dim: number }`.
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: reads `user_code, background_key, background_dim`.
  - `url = backgroundUrl(userCode, key)`: null when either is missing. Otherwise `/bg/<encodeURIComponent(userCode)>`, plus `?v=<token>` when the key's file name (minus `.webp`) contains a `-` (token = text after the last `-`).
  - `dim = clampDim(dim ?? 70)`.
- Returns: `{url, dim}`.
- Errors: none of its own; the common failure modes in 7.2.6 apply.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` when there is no session (requireUser). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: none.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:524 -> lib/background.ts:backgroundUrl/clampDim.

##### account.updateBackgroundDim
- Signature: `updateBackgroundDim(dim: number): Promise<{ dim: number }>`
- Auth: requireUser.
- Does: `value = clampDim(dim)`:
  - null, undefined or `""` give 70.
  - Other values go through `Number()`; non-finite gives 70.
  - Otherwise the value is rounded and clamped to 0..95.

  Then `UPDATE "user" SET background_dim = value`.
- Returns: `{dim: value}`.
- Errors: none of its own; the common failure modes in 7.2.6 apply.

##### account.listAdminUsers
- Signature: `listAdminUsers(): Promise<AdminUserRow[]>`
- Auth: requireAdmin.
- Does: three reads.
  1. All users, `ORDER BY created_at ASC`.
  2. `SELECT owner_id, count(*)::int FROM uploads WHERE deleted_at IS NULL GROUP BY owner_id`.
  3. `SELECT value FROM verification WHERE identifier LIKE 'invite:%' AND expires_at > now`, which gives the pending-invite set.
- Returns: one `AdminUserRow` per user:
  - `id`, `name`, `email`
  - `role` (null stays null), `banned` (null becomes false)
  - `createdAt`: ISO string or null
  - `tag`: `user_code` or null
  - `avatar`: `avatarUrl(tag, image)`. A null image gives null. A value starting with `http(s)://` or `/` is passed through. An `avatars/` key with a tag gives `/avatar/<enc tag>[?v=<token>]`. Anything else gives null.
  - `usedQuota` (null becomes 0), `maxQuota` (null becomes -1)
  - `files`: live uploads
  - `allowRootShortLinks`, `emailVerified` (null becomes false)
  - `invitePending`
- Errors: none of its own; the common failure modes in 7.2.6 apply.
- Redirect / notFound: `{"redirect":"/login"}` without a session, `{"redirect":"/dashboard"}` for a non-admin (requireAdmin). Never notFound.
- Revalidate: none.
- Cookies: none beyond the session maintenance in 7.2.3.
- Upstream: account.ts:593 -> lib/invites.ts:pendingInviteUserIds, lib/avatar.ts:avatarUrl, lib/admin-users.ts (type).

##### account.setUserQuota
- Signature: `setUserQuota(userId: string, bytes: number): Promise<{ ok: boolean }>`
- Auth: requireAdmin.
- Does: returns `{ok:false}` when `!Number.isFinite(bytes)` (any non-number, numeric strings included) or when `Math.round(bytes)` is not a safe integer. Otherwise `value = bytes < 0 ? -1 : round(bytes)` and `UPDATE "user" SET max_quota = value WHERE id = userId`, with no existence check.
- Returns: `{ok:true}` | `{ok:false}`.
- Errors: none of its own; the common failure modes in 7.2.6 apply.
